<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students should learn to respect opinions that differ from their own.  学生应学会尊重与自己不同的意见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 revere</td><td>/rɪˈvɪr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（尤指对神的）尊崇，尊敬</td><td>revered as a hero<br>被尊崇为英雄</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students in the village still revere the teacher who founded their school.  村里的学生仍然尊崇创办他们学校的那位老师。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 venerate</td><td>/ˈvenəreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>尊敬</td><td>venerate their ancestors<br>崇敬他们的祖先</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Villagers still venerate their ancestors with offerings twice each year.  村民每年两次以供品崇敬他们的祖先。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>soak</b></td><td>/soʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使…湿透</td><td>soak the beans overnight<br>把豆子浸泡一夜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soak the dried beans overnight before cooking them the next morning.  把干豆浸泡一夜，第二天早上再煮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 douse</td><td>/daʊs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扑灭（火或灯）；浇透</td><td>douse the flames<br>扑灭火焰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Firefighters worked all night to douse the flames on the upper floors.  消防员整夜奋战，扑灭了高层的火焰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drench</td><td>/drentʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使湿透，浸透</td><td>drenched to the skin<br>浑身湿透</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sudden storm drenched us to the skin before we reached shelter.  一场突来的暴雨在我们找到避处前就把我们浇得浑身湿透。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drown</td><td>/ˈdraʊn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>溺死；淹没</td><td>drown out the noise<br>把噪音压过去</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Loud applause drowned out the speaker's closing words completely.  响亮的掌声完全淹没了演讲者的结束语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impregnate</td><td>/ɪmˈpreɡneɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使浸透，使充满；使受孕</td><td>impregnate cloth with oil<br>使布浸透油</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The wood is impregnated with resin so that it resists water.  这种木材浸透了树脂，因此能防水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 macerate</td><td>/ˈmæsəreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>浸泡（以软化）</td><td>macerate fruit in wine<br>用酒浸软水果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The chef macerated the fruit in sweet wine for several hours.  厨师把水果在甜酒中浸软了好几个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 saturate</td><td>/ˈsætʃəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使饱和，浸透</td><td>saturate the sponge<br>使海绵吸饱水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy rain had saturated the field before the match even began.  比赛还没开始，大雨已浸透了球场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sodden</td><td>/ˈsɑːdn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>湿透的</td><td>sodden ground<br>湿透的地面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our boots sank into the sodden ground after three days of rain.  连下三天雨后，我们的靴子陷进湿透的地里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sop</td><td>/sɑːp/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>浸湿；吸干（液体）；安慰物</td><td>sop up the spilled milk<br>吸干洒出的牛奶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He used a towel to sop up the milk spilled on the floor.  他用毛巾吸干洒在地上的牛奶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 souse</td><td>/saʊs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>浸泡；浇透</td><td>souse herring in brine<br>把鲱鱼泡在盐水里</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She soused the vegetables in cold water before cooking them.  她做菜前把蔬菜泡在冷水里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 steep</td><td>/stiːp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>浸泡，浸渍</td><td>steep tea leaves in hot water<br>把茶叶泡在热水里</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Steep the dried leaves in boiling water for about ten minutes.  把干叶子在沸水里浸泡约十分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dehydrate</td><td>/diːˈhaɪdreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>去除水分，使干燥；使失去活力</td><td>dehydrate fruit for storage<br>把水果脱水储存</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Campers dehydrate fruit and meat so the food lasts for weeks.  露营者把水果和肉脱水，好让食物保存数周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dry</td><td>/ˈdraɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>干的，干燥的；使干燥</td><td>bone dry soil<br>干透的土壤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Hang the towels outside until they are completely dry.  把毛巾挂到外面直到完全干燥。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 desiccate</del></td><td>/ˈdesɪkeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使干燥，使脱水</td><td>desiccate the soil completely<br>使土壤完全干透</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Months without rain desiccated the fields and killed the young corn.  数月无雨使田地干透，幼小的玉米也枯死了。</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sober</b></td><td>/ˈsoʊbər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严肃的；节制的</td><td>a sober assessment<br>冷静的评估</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 earnest</td><td>/ˈɜːrnɪst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严肃认真的</td><td>an earnest student<br>认真的学生</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳近 sedate</td><td>/sɪˈdeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>淡定的，安静的</td><td>a sedate atmosphere<br>安静沉稳的气氛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hotel keeps a sedate atmosphere that older guests clearly appreciate.  这家酒店保持着安静沉稳的气氛，年长客人显然很喜欢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 severe</td><td>/səˈvɪr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严厉的；严重的</td><td>a severe penalty<br>严厉的处罚</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flip</td><td>/ˈflɪp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>轻率的，不恭的；轻抛</td><td>a flip answer<br>轻率的回答</td></tr>

<tr><td colspan="7">例 She regretted her flippant comment about the accident.  她后悔自己对那场事故的轻率评论。</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳反 jocular</td><td>/ˈdʒɑːkjələr/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>搞笑的，欢乐的</td><td>a jocular remark<br>诙谐的话</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 Joking</td><td>/ˈdʒoʊkɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>开玩笑的，戏谑的</td><td>in a joking tone<br>以戏谑的语气</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hedonistic</td><td>/ˌhiːdəˈnɪstɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>享乐主义的，纵情享乐的</td><td>a hedonistic lifestyle<br>享乐主义的生活</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳反 self-indulgent</td><td>/ˌsɛlf ɪnˈdʌldʒənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>放纵自己的，纵情享乐的</td><td>a self-indulgent lifestyle<br>放纵享乐的生活方式</td></tr>

<tr><td><del>↳反 voluptuary</del></td><td>/vəˈlʌptʃueri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>酒色之徒；纵欲的</td><td>an idle voluptuary<br>游手好闲的酒色之徒</td></tr>

<tbody>

<tr><td>↳近 awash</td><td>/əˈwɔːʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（如洪水般）泛滥的</td><td>awash with cash<br>现金泛滥</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 logged</td><td>/ˈlɔːɡd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>浸满水的；已记录的</td><td>a logged riverbank<br>浸满水的河岸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The logged field could not be plowed for weeks.  这片浸满水的田地数周都无法耕种。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 saturated</td><td>/ˈsætʃəreɪtɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>浸透的，饱和的</td><td>saturated with rain<br>被雨水浸透</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The soil was completely saturated after three days of rain.  下了三天雨后土壤完全浸透了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 soaked</td><td>/ˈsoʊkt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>湿透的，浸泡过的</td><td>soaked through<br>彻底湿透</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her shoes were soaked through by the time she got home.  她到家时鞋子已彻底湿透。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 watered</td><td>/ˈwɔːtərd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>浸水的；掺水的</td><td>well watered ground<br>浸透水的地面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The freshly watered lawn glistened in the morning light.  刚浇过水的草坪在晨光中闪着光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 waterlogged</td><td>/ˈwɔːtərˌlɑːɡd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>浸满水的，水淹的</td><td>a waterlogged pitch<br>浸满水的球场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The match was cancelled because the pitch was waterlogged.  因为球场被水浸透，比赛被取消。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 douse</td><td>/daʊs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扑灭（火或灯）；浇透</td><td>douse the flames<br>扑灭火焰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Firefighters worked all night to douse the flames on the upper floors.  消防员整夜奋战，扑灭了高层的火焰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drench</td><td>/drentʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使湿透，浸透</td><td>drenched to the skin<br>浑身湿透</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sudden storm drenched us to the skin before we reached shelter.  一场突来的暴雨在我们找到避处前就把我们浇得浑身湿透。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drown</td><td>/ˈdraʊn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>溺死；淹没</td><td>drown out the noise<br>把噪音压过去</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Loud applause drowned out the speaker's closing words completely.  响亮的掌声完全淹没了演讲者的结束语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impregnate</td><td>/ɪmˈpreɡneɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使浸透，使充满；使受孕</td><td>impregnate cloth with oil<br>使布浸透油</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The wood is impregnated with resin so that it resists water.  这种木材浸透了树脂，因此能防水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 macerate</td><td>/ˈmæsəreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>浸泡（以软化）</td><td>macerate fruit in wine<br>用酒浸软水果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The chef macerated the fruit in sweet wine for several hours.  厨师把水果在甜酒中浸软了好几个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 saturate</td><td>/ˈsætʃəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使饱和，浸透</td><td>saturate the sponge<br>使海绵吸饱水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy rain had saturated the field before the match even began.  比赛还没开始，大雨已浸透了球场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 soak</td><td>/soʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使…湿透</td><td>soak the beans overnight<br>把豆子浸泡一夜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soak the dried beans overnight before cooking them the next morning.  把干豆浸泡一夜，第二天早上再煮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sop</td><td>/sɑːp/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>浸湿；吸干（液体）；安慰物</td><td>sop up the spilled milk<br>吸干洒出的牛奶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He used a towel to sop up the milk spilled on the floor.  他用毛巾吸干洒在地上的牛奶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 souse</td><td>/saʊs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>浸泡；浇透</td><td>souse herring in brine<br>把鲱鱼泡在盐水里</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She soused the vegetables in cold water before cooking them.  她做菜前把蔬菜泡在冷水里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 steep</td><td>/stiːp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>浸泡，浸渍</td><td>steep tea leaves in hot water<br>把茶叶泡在热水里</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Steep the dried leaves in boiling water for about ten minutes.  把干叶子在沸水里浸泡约十分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 arid</td><td>/ˈærɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>枯燥无趣的；干燥的</td><td>an arid lecture<br>枯燥的讲座</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor's arid lecture emptied half the seats before the break.  这位教授枯燥乏味的讲座在课间前就空了一半座位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dry</td><td>/ˈdraɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>干的，干燥的；使干燥</td><td>bone dry soil<br>干透的土壤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Hang the towels outside until they are completely dry.  把毛巾挂到外面直到完全干燥。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 8

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>solace</b></td><td>/ˈsɑːləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>安慰</td><td>find solace in music<br>在音乐中找到安慰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She found solace in music during the long months of her illness.  在漫长的病中，她在音乐里找到安慰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consolation</td><td>/ˌkɑːnsəˈleɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>安慰，慰藉</td><td>offer some consolation<br>给予一些安慰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her letters were his only consolation during those months.  那几个月里她的信是他唯一的慰藉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 relief</td><td>/rɪˈliːf/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>宽慰，缓解；救济</td><td>a sense of relief<br>宽慰之感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 News of her recovery brought the family enormous relief.  她康复的消息给全家带来极大宽慰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cheer</td><td>/ˈtʃɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>欢呼，喝彩；使高兴</td><td>a loud cheer<br>一阵响亮的欢呼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A loud cheer rose from the crowd when the goal was scored.  进球时人群中响起一阵响亮的欢呼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 comfort</td><td>/ˈkʌmfərt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>舒适；安慰</td><td>live in comfort<br>过舒适的生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old chair gave him a great deal of comfort.  那把旧椅子给他带来极大的舒适。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 console</td><td>/kənˈsoʊl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，慰藉</td><td>console a grieving friend<br>安慰悲伤的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing anyone said could console her that evening.  那天晚上无论谁说什么都无法安慰她。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 soothe</td><td>/suːð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，抚慰；减轻</td><td>soothe a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soft music helped soothe the restless patient through the long night.  轻柔的音乐帮助安抚了焦躁的病人度过漫长的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 agonize</td><td>/ˈæɡənaɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）非常痛苦，苦苦思索</td><td>agonize over a decision<br>为决定苦苦纠结</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She agonized for weeks over whether to accept the job.  她为是否接受这份工作苦恼了好几周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 distress</td><td>/dɪsˈtres/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>痛苦，忧伤；使痛苦</td><td>cause great distress<br>造成极大痛苦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden news distressed everyone in the small office.  突来的消息让小办公室里的每个人都很难受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 harrow</td><td>/ˈhæroʊ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>折磨，使苦恼</td><td>harrow his conscience<br>折磨他的良心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The memory of that night harrowed him for the rest of his life.  那一夜的记忆折磨了他的余生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 torment</td><td>/tɔːrˈment/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>折磨，使痛苦；痛苦</td><td>torment the prisoners<br>折磨囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Guilt tormented him for years after the accident.  事故之后，负罪感折磨了他多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 torture</td><td>/ˈtɔːrtʃər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>折磨，拷打；酷刑</td><td>under torture<br>在酷刑之下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long wait for results was pure torture for her.  漫长等待结果对她来说简直是折磨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 trouble</td><td>/ˈtrʌbəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>麻烦，打扰；使烦恼</td><td>trouble you for a moment<br>打扰你一会儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 May I trouble you for directions to the train station?  能麻烦你告诉我去火车站的路吗？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>solder</b></td><td>/ˈsɑːdər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>焊接；连接，接合</td><td>solder the parts together<br>把部件焊接在一起</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The technician soldered the loose parts together with remarkable patience.  技师极有耐心地把松脱的部件焊接在一起。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 associate</td><td>/əˈsoʊʃieɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>联合，结交；联想</td><td>associate with the group<br>与该团体结交</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He refused to associate himself with such a risky plan.  他拒绝与这样冒险的计划有牵连。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coalesce</td><td>/ˌkoʊəˈles/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>合并，融合</td><td>coalesce into a group<br>合并成一个团体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scattered protests coalesced into a national movement.  分散的抗议汇合成一场全国性运动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 combine</td><td>/kəmˈbaɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>结合，联合</td><td>combine two elements<br>结合两种元素</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The recipe combines sweet and sour flavors very successfully.  这道菜谱把甜味和酸味结合得很成功。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conjoin</td><td>/kənˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>结合，连接</td><td>conjoin the two parts<br>把两部分接合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bridge conjoins the island and the mainland neatly.  这座桥把小岛与大陆巧妙相连。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conjugate</td><td>/ˈkɑːndʒəɡeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>结合，配对；成对的</td><td>conjugate two molecules<br>使两个分子结合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers conjugate the drug to an antibody for precise delivery.  研究者把药物与抗体结合以实现精准投递。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 connect</td><td>/kəˈnekt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>连接，联结</td><td>connect the two wires<br>连接两根导线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A narrow tunnel connects the two halves of the building.  一条窄隧道把大楼的两半连起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 couple</td><td>/ˈkʌpəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>连接，结合；一对</td><td>couple the two carriages<br>连接两节车厢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Workers coupled the new carriage to the waiting train.  工人把新车厢挂到等候的列车上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fuse</td><td>/ˈfjuːz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>融合，熔合</td><td>fuse two traditions<br>融合两种传统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her music fuses jazz rhythms with traditional folk melodies.  她的音乐把爵士节奏与传统民谣旋律融为一体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 interfuse</del></td><td>/ˌɪntərˈfjuz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使混合，交融</td><td>interfuse the two styles<br>交融两种风格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The composer interfuses folk melodies with modern harmony.  作曲家把民间旋律与现代和声交融在一起。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 join</td><td>/ˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>连接，接合；加入</td><td>join the two pieces<br>把两块接起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Join the ends of the rope with a firm knot.  用牢固的结把绳子两端接起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 link</td><td>/ˈlɪŋk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>联系，纽带；连接</td><td>a close link<br>紧密的联系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers found a close link between sleep and memory.  研究者发现睡眠与记忆之间存在紧密联系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 marry</td><td>/ˈmeri/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>结合，使联合；结婚</td><td>marry tradition with innovation<br>把传统与创新结合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The design marries traditional craft with modern materials.  这个设计把传统工艺与现代材料结合起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unify</td><td>/ˈjuːnəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>统一，使成一体</td><td>unify the country<br>统一国家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A common language helped unify the scattered tribes into one nation.  共同的语言帮助把分散的部落统一为一个民族。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disassociate</td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈsoʊʃieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>分离，使脱离关系</td><td>disassociate oneself from<br>与…划清界限</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party moved to disassociate itself from the remarks.  该党着手与那些言论划清界限。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disconnect</td><td>/dɪskəˈnekt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>断开，使分离</td><td>disconnect the power<br>切断电源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please disconnect the printer before you open its back panel.  打开后盖前请先断开打印机的连接。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 disjoin</del></td><td>/dɪsˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使分开，使分离</td><td>disjoin the two parts<br>把两部分分开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The engineer had to disjoin the pipes to replace a valve.  工程师得把管子分开才能更换阀门。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 divide</td><td>/dɪˈvaɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>分开，划分；分歧</td><td>divide into groups<br>分成小组</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher divided the class into four groups of five.  老师把班级分成四个五人小组。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sever</td><td>/ˈsevər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>切断，断绝</td><td>sever all ties<br>断绝一切关系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company severed all ties with its former supplier.  公司与前供应商断绝了一切关系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 split</td><td>/ˈsplɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>分裂，分开</td><td>split into groups<br>分成小组</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee split into two camps over the proposal.  委员会因该提案分裂成两派。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sunder</td><td>/ˈsʌndər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>分开，割断</td><td>sunder the alliance<br>使联盟破裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bitter dispute sundered an alliance that had lasted decades.  这场激烈的争端割断了维持数十年的联盟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>solemnity</b></td><td>/səˈlemnəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>庄严，严肃</td><td>with due solemnity<br>以应有的庄严</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coronation was conducted with all the solemnity the occasion demanded.  加冕礼以这一场合所要求的全部庄严举行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 earnest</td><td>/ˈɜːrnɪst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严肃认真的</td><td>an earnest student<br>认真的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An earnest student stayed behind to ask about the summer reading list.  一名认真的学生留下来询问暑期阅读书目。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 graveness</del></td><td>/ˈɡreɪvnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>严肃，庄重</td><td>the graveness of his tone<br>他语气的严肃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The graveness of his tone silenced the whole room.  他语气的严肃让整个房间安静下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gravity</td><td>/ˈɡrævəti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>严肃，庄重；重力</td><td>the gravity of the situation<br>形势的严峻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nobody seemed to grasp the gravity of the situation.  似乎没人明白形势的严峻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 intentness</del></td><td>/ɪnˈtɛntnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>专注，专心</td><td>listen with intentness<br>专注地聆听</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She studied the painting with an intentness that surprised us.  她以令我们惊讶的专注端详那幅画。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 seriousness</td><td>/ˈsɪriəsnəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>严肃，认真；严重性</td><td>the seriousness of the charge<br>指控的严重性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She explained the seriousness of the charge to her client.  她向委托人说明了指控的严重性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sobriety</td><td>/səˈbraɪəti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>清醒节制；严肃</td><td>a mood of sobriety<br>严肃的气氛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He has kept his sobriety for more than ten years.  他已保持戒酒清醒十多年了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 solemnness</del></td><td>/ˈsɑləmnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>庄严，严肃</td><td>the solemnness of the oath<br>誓言的庄严</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The solemnness of the ceremony moved even the children.  仪式的庄严甚至打动了孩子们。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 staidness</del></td><td>/ˈsteɪdnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>稳重，一本正经</td><td>the staidness of the ceremony<br>仪式的庄重稳重</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The staidness of the old bank appealed to cautious clients.  这家老银行的稳重作风吸引了谨慎的客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 facetiousness</del></td><td>/fəˈsiːʃəsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不合时宜的诙谐，轻浮</td><td>unwelcome facetiousness<br>不受欢迎的玩笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His facetiousness at the funeral offended nearly everyone present.  他在葬礼上的轻浮玩笑几乎冒犯了所有在场者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 flightiness</del></td><td>/ˈflaɪtinəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>轻浮，反复无常</td><td>youthful flightiness<br>年轻人的轻浮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her flightiness made her a poor choice for the job.  她的轻浮反复使她不适合这份工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flippancy</td><td>/ˈflɪpənsi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>轻率；轻浮无礼</td><td>treat it with flippancy<br>以轻浮态度对待</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His flippancy during the memorial upset the grieving family.  他在悼念仪式上的轻浮无礼让悲伤的家人很难受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 frivolity</td><td>/frəˈvɑːləti/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>轻浮，无聊之举</td><td>a moment of frivolity<br>一时的轻浮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The principal had no tolerance for frivolity in class.  校长绝不容忍课堂上的轻浮举动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 frivolousness</del></td><td>/ˈfrɪvələsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>轻浮，无聊琐碎</td><td>sheer frivolousness<br>纯粹的轻浮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge criticized the frivolousness of the entire lawsuit.  法官批评了整场诉讼的无聊琐碎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 levity</td><td>/ˈlevəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>轻浮</td><td>a moment of levity<br>一时的轻松打趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A moment of levity relieved the tension in the crowded waiting room.  一时的轻松打趣缓解了拥挤候诊室里的紧张气氛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lightheartedness</td><td>/ˌlaɪtˈhɑrtɪdnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>轻松愉快，无忧无虑</td><td>a mood of lightheartedness<br>轻松愉快的心情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was a lightheartedness in the office before the holiday.  假期前办公室里气氛轻松愉快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 play</td><td>/ˈpleɪ/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>玩耍，嬉戏；戏剧</td><td>children at play<br>玩耍中的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The garden was full of children at play that afternoon.  那天下午花园里满是玩耍的孩子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>solicitous</b></td><td>/səˈlɪsɪtəs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>为他人操心的，关切的</td><td>solicitous about her health<br>关心她的健康</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nurse was solicitous about every detail of his recovery.  护士对他康复的每个细节都很关心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 attentive</td><td>/əˈtentɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>留心的，专注的；周到的</td><td>an attentive audience<br>专注的听众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nurse stayed attentive to every change in his breathing.  护士留心他呼吸的每一点变化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 considerate</td><td>/kənˈsɪdərət/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>体谅的，考虑周到的</td><td>considerate of others<br>体谅他人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was considerate of her to call before visiting.  她来访前先打电话，很体贴周到。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 kind</td><td>/ˈkaɪnd/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>仁慈的，友好的；种类</td><td>a kind word<br>一句善意的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was kind of her to visit the sick neighbor daily.  她每天去看望生病的邻居，真是好心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 heedless</td><td>/ˈhiːdləs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不注意的，不顾的</td><td>heedless of danger<br>不顾危险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He ran into the street heedless of the traffic.  他不顾车流冲进了街道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inconsiderate</td><td>/ˌɪnkənˈsɪdərət/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不体谅他人的</td><td>inconsiderate neighbors<br>不体谅人的邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Playing loud music at midnight is simply inconsiderate.  半夜大声放音乐实在是不体谅别人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 thoughtless</td><td>/ˈθɔːtləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>欠考虑的，轻率的</td><td>a thoughtless remark<br>轻率的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was thoughtless of him to slam the door at midnight.  他半夜摔门实在是欠考虑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unthinking</td><td>/ʌnˈθɪŋkɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不体贴的，欠考虑的</td><td>an unthinking remark<br>欠考虑的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His unthinking comment hurt her more than he realized.  他那句欠考虑的话伤她比他以为的更深。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>solid</b></td><td>/ˈsɑːlɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗壮结实的</td><td>a solid, heavy build<br>粗壮结实的体格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The wrestler had a solid, heavy build unlike his slender opponent.  这名摔跤手体格粗壮结实，与瘦削的对手截然不同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 firm</td><td>/ˈfɜːrm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>坚定的，坚固的</td><td>a firm decision<br>坚定的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She remained firm despite enormous pressure from the board.  尽管董事会施加巨大压力，她依然坚定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 informed</td><td>/ɪnˈfɔːrmd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>见识广的，掌握信息的</td><td>make an informed decision<br>做出明智的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Voters need accurate data to make an informed choice.  选民需要准确的数据才能做出明智的选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 Justified</td><td>/ˈdʒʌstəˌfaɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有正当理由的，合理的</td><td>fully justified anger<br>完全合理的愤怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her complaint was justified, and the store refunded the money.  她的投诉有理，商店退了钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 levelheaded</td><td>/ˌlevlˈhedɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>头脑冷静的，明智的</td><td>a levelheaded response<br>明智冷静的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her levelheaded response to the emergency probably saved several lives that night.  她对紧急情况明智冷静的回应那晚可能救了好几条命。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 logical</td><td>/ˈlɑːdʒɪkəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>合乎逻辑的，合理的</td><td>a logical conclusion<br>合乎逻辑的结论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Selling the old machines was the logical next step.  卖掉旧机器是顺理成章的下一步。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rational</td><td>/ˈræʃnəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>理性的，合理的</td><td>a rational decision<br>理性的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Try to make a rational decision instead of an emotional one.  试着做理性而非情绪化的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reasonable</td><td>/ˈriːzənəbəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>合理的，通情达理的</td><td>a reasonable request<br>合理的请求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That seems like a perfectly reasonable request.  那似乎是完全合理的要求。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sensible</td><td>/ˈsensəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明智的，合理的</td><td>a sensible choice<br>明智的选择</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wearing boots was a sensible choice in that mud.  在那种泥地里穿靴子是明智的选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sober</td><td>/ˈsoʊbər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严肃的；节制的</td><td>a sober assessment<br>冷静的评估</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee offered a sober assessment of the country's economic prospects.  委员会对国家经济前景作出冷静的评估。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 valid</td><td>/ˈvælɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有效的；有根据的</td><td>a valid objection<br>有根据的反对意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Your ticket is valid for travel on any weekday this month.  你的票在本月任何工作日出行均有效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 well-founded</td><td>/ˌwɛl ˈfaʊndɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有充分根据的</td><td>a well-founded fear<br>有充分根据的担忧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their fear of another flood proved to be well-founded.  他们对再次洪水的担忧被证明有充分根据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 groundless</td><td>/ˈɡraʊndləs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>没有依据的</td><td>groundless accusations<br>毫无根据的指控</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court found the accusations entirely groundless and dismissed them.  法庭认定这些指控毫无根据，予以驳回。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 illogical</td><td>/ˌɪˈlɑːdʒɪkəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不合逻辑的，不合理的</td><td>an illogical conclusion<br>不合逻辑的结论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It seems illogical to cut staff while demand keeps rising.  在需求持续上升时裁员似乎不合逻辑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 invalid</td><td>/ɪnˈvælɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无效的；站不住脚的</td><td>an invalid argument<br>站不住脚的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract is invalid because it was never signed.  这份合同无效，因为从未签署。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unfounded</td><td>/ənˈfaʊndɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>毫无根据的</td><td>unfounded rumors<br>毫无根据的谣言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company called the reports of bankruptcy completely unfounded.  公司称破产的报道完全没有根据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 uninformed</td><td>/ˌʌnɪnˈfɔːrmd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不了解情况的，无知的</td><td>an uninformed opinion<br>不明情况的看法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Uninformed criticism rarely helps anyone improve their work.  不了解情况的批评很少能帮助别人改进工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unjustified</td><td>/ʌnˈdʒʌstəfaɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无正当理由的</td><td>an unjustified attack<br>无理的攻击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The harsh criticism of her work was completely unjustified.  对她工作的严厉批评完全没有道理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unreasonable</td><td>/ənˈriːznəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不合理的，过分的</td><td>unreasonable demands<br>不合理的要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Asking staff to work three weekends running is unreasonable.  要求员工连续三个周末加班是不合理的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unsound</td><td>/ənˈsaʊnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可靠的，有缺陷的</td><td>unsound reasoning<br>有缺陷的推理</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The engineer judged the old bridge structurally unsound.  工程师判定这座旧桥结构不牢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>soliloquy</b></td><td>/səˈlɪləkwi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>（尤指自言自语的）独白</td><td>Hamlet's famous soliloquy<br>哈姆雷特的著名独白</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Hamlet's famous soliloquy reveals his deepest doubts about life and death.  哈姆雷特的著名独白揭示了他对生死的深切疑问。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 monologue</td><td>/ˈmɑːnəlɔːɡ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>独白</td><td>a long monologue<br>长篇独白</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He delivered a long monologue about his childhood while everyone waited politely.  他讲了一大段关于童年的独白，众人礼貌地等着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 chorus</td><td>/ˈkɔːrəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>合唱，齐声；合唱队</td><td>sing in chorus<br>齐声合唱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villagers answered the question in chorus.  村民们齐声回答了这个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ensemble</td><td>/ɑːnˈsɑːmbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>合奏团，全体；整体</td><td>a small string ensemble<br>小型弦乐合奏团</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A small ensemble performed the piece without a conductor.  一支小型合奏团在无人指挥的情况下演奏了这首曲子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>solitude</b></td><td>/ˈsɑːlətuːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>孤独，避世</td><td>live in solitude<br>过孤独的生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After retiring, the writer lived in solitude on a remote island.  退休后，这位作家在偏远岛上过着孤独的生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aloneness</td><td>/əˈloʊnnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>孤单，独处状态</td><td>a chosen aloneness<br>自愿选择的独处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He came to value his aloneness rather than fear it.  他渐渐珍视自己的独处，而不再害怕它。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insulation</td><td>/ˌɪnsəˈleɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>隔离，隔绝；绝缘</td><td>insulation from the world<br>与世隔绝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their wealth provided insulation from ordinary hardships.  他们的财富使他们与普通苦难隔绝。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 privacy</td><td>/ˈpraɪvəsi/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>独处，隐私</td><td>in the privacy of home<br>在家中独处时</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She wanted the privacy of a quiet room to write.  她想要一间安静的房间独处写作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 seclusion</td><td>/sɪˈkluːʒən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>隐居，隔离</td><td>live in seclusion<br>过隐居生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poet spent her last years in complete seclusion.  这位诗人晚年完全隐居。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 segregation</td><td>/ˌseɡrəˈɡeɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>隔离，分离</td><td>racial segregation<br>种族隔离</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The law finally ended segregation in public schools.  这项法律最终结束了公立学校的种族隔离。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 separateness</td><td>/ˈsepərətnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>分离，独立状态</td><td>a sense of separateness<br>疏离隔绝之感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Living abroad gave him a permanent sense of separateness.  在国外生活让他有一种长久的疏离感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sequestration</td><td>/ˌsekwəˈstreɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>隔离，隐退；扣押</td><td>the sequestration of jurors<br>陪审员的隔离</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge ordered the sequestration of the jury during the trial.  法官命令在审判期间隔离陪审团。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 solitariness</del></td><td>/ˈsɑːləterinəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>孤独，独居状态</td><td>the solitariness of the hermit<br>隐士的孤独</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The solitariness of his cabin life suited him perfectly.  小木屋中孤独的生活正合他心意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 camaraderie</td><td>/ˌkɑːməˈrɑːdəri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>同伴情谊，友情</td><td>a sense of camaraderie<br>同伴间的情谊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A strong camaraderie grew among the players during the long season.  漫长的赛季让球员之间生出深厚的情谊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 companionship</td><td>/kəmˈpænjənˌʃɪp/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>陪伴，交往</td><td>seek human companionship<br>寻求人的陪伴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After years alone he began to crave companionship.  独居多年后他开始渴望有人陪伴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 company</td><td>/ˈkʌmpəni/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>陪伴，同伴；公司</td><td>keep bad company<br>结交坏朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His parents worried that he was keeping bad company at school.  他父母担心他在学校结交坏朋友。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 comradeship</td><td>/ˈkɑːmrædʃɪp/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>同志情谊，伙伴之情</td><td>wartime comradeship<br>战时的同志情谊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They remembered the comradeship of those difficult early years.  他们怀念那些艰难早年的伙伴情谊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fellowship</td><td>/ˈfeloʊˌʃɪp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>友情，团体；研究员职位</td><td>a fellowship of scholars<br>学者组成的团体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The choir offered both music and warm fellowship every week.  合唱团每周既提供音乐也带来温暖情谊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 society</td><td>/səˈsaɪəti/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>交往，社交；社会</td><td>enjoy the society of friends<br>享受与朋友交往</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He enjoyed the society of old friends more than fame.  他更喜欢与老友交往，而非名声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>solvent</b></td><td>/ˈsɑːlvənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>有偿付能力的；溶剂</td><td>remain financially solvent<br>保持有偿付能力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The small firm remained financially solvent despite the difficult trading year.  尽管经营艰难，这家小公司仍有偿付能力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bankrupt</td><td>/ˈbæŋkrʌpt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>破产的；使破产</td><td>go bankrupt<br>破产</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The factory went bankrupt after losing its largest client.  失去最大客户后，这家工厂破产了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 solute</td><td>/ˈsɑːljuːt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>溶质</td><td>dissolve the solute<br>溶解溶质</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Stir until the solute disappears completely into the water.  搅拌至溶质完全溶于水中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>somatic</b></td><td>/səˈmætɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>肉体的</td><td>somatic symptoms<br>身体上的症状</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The doctor attributed her somatic symptoms to prolonged emotional stress.  医生把她身体上的症状归因于长期的情绪压力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 animal</td><td>/ˈænəməl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>肉体的，兽性的；动物</td><td>animal instincts<br>肉体的本能</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The book explores animal appetites and moral restraint.  这本书探讨肉体欲望与道德约束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bodily</td><td>/ˈbɑːdəli/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>身体的，肉体的</td><td>bodily harm<br>身体伤害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The law protects citizens from any threat of bodily harm.  法律保护公民免受任何身体伤害的威胁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 carnal</td><td>/ˈkɑːrnl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>肉体的，物质的；世俗的</td><td>carnal pleasures<br>肉体的享乐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monk warned his students against carnal pleasures and idle talk.  僧人告诫学生远离肉体享乐和闲谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 corporal</td><td>/ˈkɔːrpərəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>肉体的，身体的；下士</td><td>corporal punishment<br>体罚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most countries have banned corporal punishment in their schools.  多数国家已禁止在学校施行体罚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 corporeal</td><td>/kɔːrˈpɔːriəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>肉体的；有形的，实体的</td><td>corporeal existence<br>肉体的存在</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ghost seemed almost corporeal in the flickering candlelight.  在摇曳的烛光中，那鬼影几乎像有实体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fleshly</td><td>/ˈflɛʃli/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>肉体的；世俗的</td><td>fleshly desires<br>肉体的欲望</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monk warned his students against fleshly temptations.  那位僧人告诫学生要远离肉体的诱惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 material</td><td>/məˈtɪriəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>物质的；重要的；材料</td><td>material comforts<br>物质上的享受</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He cared little for material wealth and lived very simply.  他不太在意物质财富，生活极为简朴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 physical</td><td>/ˈfɪzɪkəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>身体的；物质的</td><td>physical evidence<br>实物证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police found no physical evidence linking him to the crime.  警方没有找到把他与罪案联系起来的实物证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mental</td><td>/ˈmentəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精神的，心理的</td><td>mental health<br>心理健康</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Long hours took a toll on his mental health.  长时间工作损害了他的心理健康。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 spiritual</td><td>/ˈspɪrɪtʃuəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>精神的；灵魂的，宗教的</td><td>spiritual growth<br>精神成长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She turned to meditation in search of spiritual peace.  她转向冥想，以寻求精神上的平静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>somber</b></td><td>/ˈsɑːmbər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>悲伤的，不愉快的</td><td>a somber mood<br>阴郁的情绪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A somber mood settled over the town after the funeral procession passed.  送葬队伍过后，全镇陷入阴郁的气氛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 depressing</td><td>/dɪˈpresɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人沮丧的</td><td>a depressing forecast<br>令人沮丧的预测</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rainy weather and empty streets felt deeply depressing.  阴雨天气和空荡的街道让人非常压抑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dire</td><td>/ˈdaɪər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可怕的，恐怖的；迫切的</td><td>dire consequences<br>可怕的后果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report warned of dire consequences if the dam were not repaired soon.  报告警告说，若不尽快修复大坝，后果将十分可怕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dismal</td><td>/ˈdɪzməl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>阴沉的，凄凉的</td><td>a dismal failure<br>彻底的失败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dismal gray sky matched everyone's mood.  阴沉灰暗的天空与众人的心情相符。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 funereal</td><td>/fjuˈnɪriəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>阴沉的，悲哀的</td><td>a funereal atmosphere<br>阴沉悲哀的气氛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A funereal silence settled over the defeated team.  一片阴沉的寂静笼罩着落败的队伍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lugubrious</td><td>/ləˈɡuːbriəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（故作夸张的）悲哀的</td><td>a lugubrious expression<br>夸张悲哀的表情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wore a lugubrious expression whenever anyone mentioned his ruined garden.  每次有人提到他毁掉的花园，他就露出夸张悲哀的表情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 melancholy</td><td>/ˈmelənkɑːli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>忧郁，伤感</td><td>a mood of quiet melancholy<br>淡淡的忧郁情绪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Autumn rain always fills him with a vague melancholy.  秋雨总让他心里泛起一种莫名的忧郁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 miserable</td><td>/ˈmɪzərəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>痛苦的，凄惨的</td><td>feel utterly miserable<br>感到极其痛苦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cold rain made the whole camping trip miserable.  冰冷的雨让整个露营之行变得凄惨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 morbid</td><td>/ˈmɔːrbɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>病态的，阴郁不健康的</td><td>a morbid fascination<br>病态的迷恋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She developed a morbid fascination with old newspaper reports of unsolved crimes.  她对旧报纸上未解案件的报道产生了病态的迷恋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 morose</td><td>/məˈroʊs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>忧郁的</td><td>grow morose and silent<br>变得忧郁沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After losing the election he grew morose and rarely left his apartment.  落选之后他变得忧郁，很少离开公寓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 saturnine</td><td>/ˈsætərnaɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>忧郁的，阴沉的</td><td>a saturnine temperament<br>阴沉的性情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His saturnine expression discouraged anyone from starting a cheerful conversation.  他阴沉的表情让人不敢开启轻松的话题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sullen</td><td>/ˈsʌlən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>闷闷不乐的</td><td>a sullen silence<br>闷闷不乐的沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sullen silence fell over the table when the topic was raised.  提到这个话题时，餐桌上陷入闷闷不乐的沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cheerful</td><td>/ˈtʃɪrfəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>快乐的，愉快的</td><td>a cheerful greeting<br>愉快的问候</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stayed cheerful even after the long delay.  即使长时间延误后他仍保持愉快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 delighted</td><td>/dɪˈlaɪtəd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>高兴的，欣喜的</td><td>delighted with the gift<br>对礼物很欣喜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was delighted to hear about her friend's promotion.  听说朋友升职，她非常高兴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 festive</td><td>/ˈfestɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>欢乐的，节日的</td><td>a festive mood<br>欢乐的气氛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole street took on a festive air by evening.  到傍晚整条街都染上了节日气息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gay</td><td>/ˈɡeɪ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>快乐的，欢快的</td><td>gay laughter<br>欢快的笑声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Gay music and bright lanterns filled the village square that evening.  那晚欢快的音乐和明亮的灯笼充满了村中广场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 jocund</del></td><td>/ˈdʒɑːkənd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>欢快的，高兴的</td><td>a jocund gathering<br>欢快的聚会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The jocund crowd sang and danced until the last lantern went out.  欢快的人群唱歌跳舞，直到最后一盏灯笼熄灭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 jovial</td><td>/ˈdʒoʊviəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>愉快的</td><td>a jovial host<br>愉快的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our jovial host kept the guests laughing throughout the long winter evening.  我们愉快的主人让客人们在漫长的冬夜里笑声不断。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 9

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>somnolence</b></td><td>/ˈsɑːmnələns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>瞌睡，嗜睡</td><td>afternoon somnolence<br>午后的困倦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The warm lecture hall produced a general somnolence among the students.  温暖的讲堂让学生们普遍犯困。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 doziness</del></td><td>/ˈdoʊzinəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>困倦，昏昏欲睡</td><td>a pleasant doziness<br>舒服的困倦感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A warm doziness crept over him beside the fire.  炉火旁一阵温暖的困倦袭上他心头。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drowsiness</td><td>/ˈdraʊzinəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>困倦，昏睡</td><td>cause drowsiness<br>引起困倦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The medicine may cause drowsiness, so avoid driving.  这种药可能引起困倦，所以不要开车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 insomnia</td><td>/ɪnˈsɑːmniə/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>失眠</td><td>suffer from insomnia<br>患失眠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has suffered from insomnia since the accident.  自那次事故以来她一直失眠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sleeplessness</td><td>/ˈsliːpləsnəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>失眠，无法入睡</td><td>nights of sleeplessness<br>无法入睡的夜晚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Weeks of sleeplessness left her pale and forgetful.  数周的失眠让她面色苍白、记性变差。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wakefulness</td><td>/ˈweɪkfəlnəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>清醒，醒着的状态</td><td>a state of wakefulness<br>清醒的状态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Coffee prolonged his wakefulness far into the night.  咖啡让他清醒到深夜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>slapdash</b></td><td>/ˈslæpdæʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>马虎的</td><td>a slapdash job<br>马虎的活儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His report was so slapdash that the editor refused to publish it.  他的报告太马虎，编辑拒绝发表。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aimless</td><td>/ˈeɪmləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无目的的，无方向的</td><td>an aimless life<br>漫无目的的生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After graduation he drifted through several aimless years in the city.  毕业后他在城里虚度了几年漫无目的的生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arbitrary</td><td>/ˈɑːrbətreri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>专横的，独断专行的；随意的</td><td>an arbitrary decision<br>武断的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rules seemed completely arbitrary to the students.  这些规定在学生看来完全是随意的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 desultory</td><td>/ˈdesəlˌtɔːri/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>散漫的，无条理的</td><td>desultory conversation<br>散漫的谈话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They made desultory notes instead of a real research plan.  他们做了些散漫的笔记，而非真正的研究计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 erratic</td><td>/ɪˈrætɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>反复无常的，不规律的</td><td>erratic driving behavior<br>飘忽不定的驾驶行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His erratic driving behavior alarmed the passengers long before the police stopped him.  早在警察拦下他之前，他飘忽不定的驾驶行为就让乘客惊慌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 haphazard</td><td>/hæpˈhæzərd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>随意的；杂乱无章的</td><td>a haphazard manner<br>杂乱无章的方式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The files were stacked in a haphazard pile on the office floor.  文件杂乱无章地堆在办公室的地板上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stray</td><td>/streɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>漫无目的的</td><td>stray thoughts<br>零散的念头</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A few stray thoughts kept interrupting her careful reading of the contract.  几个零散的念头不断打断她对合同的细读。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 methodical</td><td>/məˈθɑːdɪkl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>井然有序的，有条理的</td><td>a methodical approach<br>有条理的方法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A methodical search of the archive finally produced the missing land records.  对档案的有条理搜查终于找出了缺失的土地记录。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 nonrandom</del></td><td>/ˌnɑnˈrændəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非随机的，有规律的</td><td>a nonrandom pattern<br>非随机的模式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nonrandom spacing of the trees suggested careful human planning.  树木非随机的间距说明这是人为精心规划的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 orderly</td><td>/ˈɔːrdərli/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有秩序的，整齐的</td><td>an orderly queue<br>整齐的队伍</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The students left the hall in an orderly line.  学生们排成整齐的队伍离开礼堂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 organized</td><td>/ˈɔːrɡəˌnaɪzd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有条理的，有组织的</td><td>highly organized files<br>条理分明的文件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She keeps an organized notebook for every course she takes.  她为每门课都备有一本条理分明的笔记。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 regular</td><td>/ˈreɡjələr/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>规律的，通常的</td><td>on a regular basis<br>定期地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He goes swimming on a regular basis every week.  他每周都定期去游泳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 systematic</td><td>/ˌsɪstəˈmætɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>系统的，有条理的</td><td>a systematic review<br>系统性回顾</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team took a systematic approach to checking each component.  团队用系统的方法逐一检查了每个部件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>soothe</b></td><td>/suːð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，抚慰；减轻</td><td>soothe a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soft music helped soothe the restless patient through the long night.  轻柔的音乐帮助安抚了焦躁的病人度过漫长的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cheer</td><td>/ˈtʃɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>欢呼，喝彩；使高兴</td><td>a loud cheer<br>一阵响亮的欢呼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A loud cheer rose from the crowd when the goal was scored.  进球时人群中响起一阵响亮的欢呼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 comfort</td><td>/ˈkʌmfərt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>舒适；安慰</td><td>live in comfort<br>过舒适的生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old chair gave him a great deal of comfort.  那把旧椅子给他带来极大的舒适。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 console</td><td>/kənˈsoʊl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，慰藉</td><td>console a grieving friend<br>安慰悲伤的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing anyone said could console her that evening.  那天晚上无论谁说什么都无法安慰她。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 solace</td><td>/ˈsɑːləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>安慰</td><td>find solace in music<br>在音乐中找到安慰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She found solace in music during the long months of her illness.  在漫长的病中，她在音乐里找到安慰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 agonize</td><td>/ˈæɡənaɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）非常痛苦，苦苦思索</td><td>agonize over a decision<br>为决定苦苦纠结</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She agonized for weeks over whether to accept the job.  她为是否接受这份工作苦恼了好几周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 distress</td><td>/dɪsˈtres/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>痛苦，忧伤；使痛苦</td><td>cause great distress<br>造成极大痛苦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden news distressed everyone in the small office.  突来的消息让小办公室里的每个人都很难受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 harrow</td><td>/ˈhæroʊ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>折磨，使苦恼</td><td>harrow his conscience<br>折磨他的良心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The memory of that night harrowed him for the rest of his life.  那一夜的记忆折磨了他的余生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 torment</td><td>/tɔːrˈment/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>折磨，使痛苦；痛苦</td><td>torment the prisoners<br>折磨囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Guilt tormented him for years after the accident.  事故之后，负罪感折磨了他多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 torture</td><td>/ˈtɔːrtʃər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>折磨，拷打；酷刑</td><td>under torture<br>在酷刑之下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long wait for results was pure torture for her.  漫长等待结果对她来说简直是折磨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 trouble</td><td>/ˈtrʌbəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>麻烦，打扰；使烦恼</td><td>trouble you for a moment<br>打扰你一会儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 May I trouble you for directions to the train station?  能麻烦你告诉我去火车站的路吗？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sop</b></td><td>/sɑːp/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>浸湿；吸干（液体）；安慰物</td><td>sop up the spilled milk<br>吸干洒出的牛奶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He used a towel to sop up the milk spilled on the floor.  他用毛巾吸干洒在地上的牛奶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 balm</td><td>/bɑːm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>香油，止痛膏；安慰物</td><td>a balm for grief<br>抚慰悲伤之物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her quiet letters were a balm for his grief that winter.  那年冬天，她安静的信件是抚慰他悲伤之物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 emollient</td><td>/iˈmɑːliənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>起缓和作用的</td><td>an emollient tone of voice<br>缓和气氛的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He answered in an emollient tone of voice that cooled the argument.  他用缓和的语气回答，让争吵冷了下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 placebo</td><td>/pləˈsiːboʊ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>安慰性的事物</td><td>a mere placebo<br>仅是安慰性的东西</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The promise of a review was a mere placebo for worried residents.  承诺复查对忧心的居民只是安慰性的东西。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 salve</td><td>/sæv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>减轻，缓解</td><td>salve one's conscience<br>减轻内心的不安</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave money to the school to salve his uneasy conscience.  他向学校捐钱，以减轻内心的不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unguent</td><td>/ˈʌŋɡwənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>油膏，药膏</td><td>apply a soothing unguent<br>涂上安抚的药膏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nurse spread a cooling unguent over the burn.  护士在烧伤处涂上清凉的药膏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 douse</td><td>/daʊs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扑灭（火或灯）；浇透</td><td>douse the flames<br>扑灭火焰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Firefighters worked all night to douse the flames on the upper floors.  消防员整夜奋战，扑灭了高层的火焰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drench</td><td>/drentʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使湿透，浸透</td><td>drenched to the skin<br>浑身湿透</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sudden storm drenched us to the skin before we reached shelter.  一场突来的暴雨在我们找到避处前就把我们浇得浑身湿透。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drown</td><td>/ˈdraʊn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>溺死；淹没</td><td>drown out the noise<br>把噪音压过去</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Loud applause drowned out the speaker's closing words completely.  响亮的掌声完全淹没了演讲者的结束语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impregnate</td><td>/ɪmˈpreɡneɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使浸透，使充满；使受孕</td><td>impregnate cloth with oil<br>使布浸透油</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The wood is impregnated with resin so that it resists water.  这种木材浸透了树脂，因此能防水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 macerate</td><td>/ˈmæsəreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>浸泡（以软化）</td><td>macerate fruit in wine<br>用酒浸软水果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The chef macerated the fruit in sweet wine for several hours.  厨师把水果在甜酒中浸软了好几个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 saturate</td><td>/ˈsætʃəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使饱和，浸透</td><td>saturate the sponge<br>使海绵吸饱水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy rain had saturated the field before the match even began.  比赛还没开始，大雨已浸透了球场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 soak</td><td>/soʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使…湿透</td><td>soak the beans overnight<br>把豆子浸泡一夜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soak the dried beans overnight before cooking them the next morning.  把干豆浸泡一夜，第二天早上再煮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sodden</td><td>/ˈsɑːdn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>湿透的</td><td>sodden ground<br>湿透的地面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our boots sank into the sodden ground after three days of rain.  连下三天雨后，我们的靴子陷进湿透的地里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 souse</td><td>/saʊs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>浸泡；浇透</td><td>souse herring in brine<br>把鲱鱼泡在盐水里</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She soused the vegetables in cold water before cooking them.  她做菜前把蔬菜泡在冷水里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 steep</td><td>/stiːp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>浸泡，浸渍</td><td>steep tea leaves in hot water<br>把茶叶泡在热水里</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Steep the dried leaves in boiling water for about ten minutes.  把干叶子在沸水里浸泡约十分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 irritant</td><td>/ˈɪrɪtənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>刺激物；恼人的事物</td><td>a constant irritant<br>持续的烦扰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cigarette smoke is a serious irritant to sensitive eyes.  香烟烟雾对敏感的眼睛是严重的刺激物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stimulant</td><td>/ˈstɪmjələnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>兴奋剂，刺激物</td><td>a mild stimulant<br>温和的兴奋剂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Coffee acts as a stimulant for most healthy adults.  对大多数健康成年人来说咖啡起兴奋剂作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dehydrate</td><td>/diːˈhaɪdreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>去除水分，使干燥；使失去活力</td><td>dehydrate fruit for storage<br>把水果脱水储存</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Campers dehydrate fruit and meat so the food lasts for weeks.  露营者把水果和肉脱水，好让食物保存数周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dry</td><td>/ˈdraɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>干的，干燥的；使干燥</td><td>bone dry soil<br>干透的土壤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Hang the towels outside until they are completely dry.  把毛巾挂到外面直到完全干燥。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 desiccate</del></td><td>/ˈdesɪkeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使干燥，使脱水</td><td>desiccate the soil completely<br>使土壤完全干透</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Months without rain desiccated the fields and killed the young corn.  数月无雨使田地干透，幼小的玉米也枯死了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>sophism</b></del></td><td>/ˈsɑːfɪzəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>假推理，诡辩</td><td>expose the sophism<br>揭穿这种诡辩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor patiently exposed the sophism hidden in the student's argument.  教授耐心揭穿了学生论证中隐藏的诡辩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sophistry</td><td>/ˈsɑːfɪstri/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>诡辩，似是而非的推理</td><td>clever sophistry<br>巧妙的诡辩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His argument was clever sophistry rather than honest reasoning.  他的论证是巧妙的诡辩，而非诚实的推理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sophisticated</b></td><td>/səˈfɪstɪkeɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>复杂精密的；老练的</td><td>a sophisticated system<br>复杂精密的系统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lab uses highly sophisticated equipment to detect the particles.  实验室用极为精密的设备探测这些粒子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cosmopolitan</td><td>/ˌkɑːzməˈpɑːlɪtən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>有世界性眼光的，包容的</td><td>a cosmopolitan city<br>国际化的都市</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years abroad gave her a cosmopolitan outlook her old classmates clearly lacked.  多年国外生活给了她老同学明显缺乏的世界性眼光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 debonair</td><td>/ˌdebəˈner/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>风度翩翩的，温文尔雅的</td><td>a debonair manner<br>从容优雅的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The debonair gentleman charmed every woman in the crowded ballroom.  这位风度翩翩的绅士让拥挤舞厅里的每位女士都为之倾倒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 knowing</td><td>/ˈnoʊɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>故意的，明知的</td><td>a knowing violation of the rules<br>明知故犯的违规</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge called it a knowing violation of the safety rules.  法官称这是明知故犯地违反安全规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 smart</td><td>/ˈsmɑːrt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>聪明的；时髦的</td><td>a smart move<br>明智之举</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Buying the tickets early was a very smart decision.  提前买票是个非常明智的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 suave</td><td>/ˈswɑːv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>老于世故的，圆滑的</td><td>a suave diplomat<br>圆滑的外交官</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The suave diplomat deflected every awkward question gracefully.  这位圆滑的外交官优雅地挡开了每个尴尬问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 urbane</td><td>/ərˈbeɪn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>彬彬有礼的，文雅的</td><td>an urbane host<br>文雅的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our urbane host guided the conversation with easy wit and grace.  我们文雅的主人以轻松的机智和风度引导着谈话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 worldly</td><td>/ˈwɜːrldli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>世间的，世俗的；老练的</td><td>worldly possessions<br>世俗财产</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave away all his worldly possessions and entered the monastery.  他捐出全部世俗财产，进了修道院。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 baroque</td><td>/bəˈroʊk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>装饰华丽的，过分雕琢的，复杂的</td><td>baroque ornamentation<br>华丽繁复的装饰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ceiling was covered with heavy baroque ornamentation in gold.  天花板上覆满了华丽繁复的金色装饰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 byzantine</td><td>/ˈbɪzəntiːn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>错综复杂的</td><td>byzantine bureaucracy<br>错综复杂的官僚体制</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tax code is so byzantine that even experts disagree.  税法错综复杂，连专家都各执一词。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 complicated</td><td>/ˈkɑːmplɪkeɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>复杂的，难懂的</td><td>a complicated process<br>复杂的流程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tax rules are so complicated that most people hire help.  税务规定如此复杂，多数人都请人帮忙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 convoluted</td><td>/ˈkɑːnvəluːtɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>复杂的，费解的</td><td>a convoluted argument<br>费解的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The instructions were so convoluted that nobody could assemble the shelf.  说明书太过费解，没人能装好那个架子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 elaborate</td><td>/ɪˈlæbəreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>详细阐述；精心制作的</td><td>elaborate on a point<br>详述某一点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Could you elaborate on your last point a little?  你能就最后一点再详细说明一下吗？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intricate</td><td>/ˈɪntrəkət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>错综复杂的，精细的</td><td>an intricate pattern<br>精细的图案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The carpet shows an intricate pattern of leaves and birds.  地毯上是精细复杂的叶与鸟图案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 involved</td><td>/ɪnˈvɑːlvd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>错综复杂的，难懂的</td><td>an involved argument<br>错综复杂的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plot is so involved that few readers finish the book.  情节太过错综复杂，很少有读者读完这本书。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 knotty</td><td>/ˈnɑːti/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>复杂的，困难的</td><td>a knotty question<br>棘手的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor posed a knotty question that nobody could answer confidently.  教授提出了一个没人能自信回答的棘手问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 labyrinthine</td><td>/ˌlæbəˈrɪnθaɪn/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迷宫似的，复杂曲折的</td><td>labyrinthine corridors<br>迷宫般的走廊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We got lost in the labyrinthine corridors of the old hospital building.  我们在这座老医院大楼迷宫般的走廊里迷了路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tangled</td><td>/ˈtæŋɡəld/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>纠缠的，错综复杂的</td><td>a tangled web<br>错综复杂的关系网</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The case involves a tangled web of loans and false names.  此案涉及贷款与假名交织的复杂网络。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 guileless</td><td>/ˈɡaɪlləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>单纯的，不狡诈的</td><td>a guileless smile<br>天真无邪的笑容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her guileless smile made everyone in the room trust her immediately.  她天真无邪的笑容让屋里所有人立刻信任她。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ingenuous</td><td>/ɪnˈdʒenjuəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>天真淳朴的</td><td>an ingenuous young student<br>天真淳朴的年轻学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His ingenuous questions about the city amused the veteran taxi driver greatly.  他关于这座城市的天真提问让老出租车司机大为好笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 innocent</td><td>/ˈɪnəsənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无害的，无恶意的；无辜的</td><td>an innocent remark<br>无恶意的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 What began as an innocent joke ended in a serious quarrel.  一句本无恶意的玩笑最后引发了激烈争吵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 naive</td><td>/naɪˈiːv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>天真的，幼稚的</td><td>naive assumptions<br>天真的假设</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was naive to expect honesty from someone with that record.  指望有那种记录的人诚实太天真了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 plain</td><td>/ˈpleɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>完全的，十足的；朴素的，清楚的</td><td>plain stupid<br>简直愚蠢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ignoring the warning was just plain stupid.  忽视警告纯粹是愚蠢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 simple</td><td>/ˈsɪmpəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>简单的，容易的</td><td>a simple task<br>简单的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Follow these simple steps and the machine will restart.  按这些简单步骤操作，机器就会重启。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>soporific</b></td><td>/ˌsɑːpəˈrɪfɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>催眠的；安眠药</td><td>a mild soporific<br>温和的安眠药</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Alcohol acts as a mild soporific, but it ruins deep sleep.  酒精是一种温和的催眠物，但会破坏深度睡眠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hypnotic</td><td>/hɪpˈnɑːtɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>催眠的；迷人的；催眠药</td><td>a hypnotic rhythm<br>催眠般的节奏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The drummer kept a hypnotic rhythm that held everyone completely still.  鼓手保持着一种催眠般的节奏，让所有人一动不动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 narcotic</td><td>/nɑrˈkɑːtɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>催眠的；麻醉的</td><td>a narcotic effect<br>催眠的效果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The steady rain had an almost narcotic effect on us.  连绵的雨对我们几乎有催眠的效果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 opiate</td><td>/ˈoʊpiət/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>催眠药；麻醉剂</td><td>an opiate for the mind<br>心灵的麻醉剂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He called cheap television an opiate for tired workers.  他把廉价电视称作疲惫工人的麻醉剂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 slumberous</del></td><td>/ˈslʌmbərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>昏昏欲睡的，催眠的</td><td>a slumberous afternoon<br>令人困倦的午后</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The slumberous heat of noon emptied every street in the village.  正午令人昏睡的暑气让村里每条街都空了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 somniferous</del></td><td>/sɑːmˈnɪfərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>催眠的，使人昏睡的</td><td>a somniferous lecture<br>令人昏睡的讲座</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor's somniferous voice put half the class to sleep.  教授催眠般的声音让半个班的人睡着了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 somnolent</td><td>/ˈsɑːmnələnt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>困倦的；催眠的</td><td>a somnolent afternoon<br>令人昏昏欲睡的午后</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The somnolent heat of the village slowed everything down.  村里令人昏睡的暑气让一切都慢了下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stimulant</td><td>/ˈstɪmjələnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>兴奋剂，刺激物</td><td>a mild stimulant<br>温和的兴奋剂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Coffee acts as a stimulant for most healthy adults.  对大多数健康成年人来说咖啡起兴奋剂作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sordid</b></td><td>/ˈsɔːrdɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>肮脏的，不干净的；卑鄙的</td><td>a sordid little room<br>肮脏的小房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They lived for years in a sordid little room without heat.  他们在没有暖气的肮脏小屋里住了多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 besmirched</td><td>/bɪˈsmɜrtʃt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>被弄脏的；名声受损的</td><td>a besmirched reputation<br>受损的名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One careless lie left his besmirched name beyond repair.  一句大意的谎言让他受损的名声无法挽回。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dirty</td><td>/ˈdɜːrti/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>肮脏的；卑鄙的</td><td>dirty windows<br>脏污的窗子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children came home with dirty hands and happy faces.  孩子们回家时手很脏，脸上却很开心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dusty</td><td>/ˈdʌsti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>满是灰尘的</td><td>dusty old books<br>满是灰尘的旧书</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We opened dusty boxes in the attic all afternoon.  我们整个下午在阁楼打开满是灰尘的箱子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 filthy</td><td>/ˈfɪlθi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>污秽的，极脏的</td><td>filthy old clothes<br>污秽的旧衣服</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The kitchen floor was filthy after the long noisy party.  漫长喧闹的聚会后厨房地板脏得一塌糊涂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 foul</td><td>/ˈfaʊl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>玷污（名誉）；弄脏</td><td>foul one's good name<br>玷污自己的好名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scandal fouled the good name he had spent decades building.  这桩丑闻玷污了他数十年积累的好名声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grubby</td><td>/ˈɡrʌbi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>肮脏的；卑劣的</td><td>grubby hands<br>脏兮兮的手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The boy came home with grubby knees and a torn shirt.  男孩回家时膝盖脏兮兮，衬衫也破了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 muddy</td><td>/ˈmʌdi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>浑浊的，不清晰的；使难以理解</td><td>muddy the argument<br>把论点搅浑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Vague statistics only muddy the argument instead of settling it.  含糊的统计数据只会把论点搅浑，而不能解决争论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nasty</td><td>/ˈnæsti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人厌恶的，肮脏的；恶劣的</td><td>a nasty smell<br>难闻的气味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A nasty stain spread slowly across the pale carpet.  一块难看的污渍在浅色地毯上慢慢蔓延。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 smudged</td><td>/smʌdʒd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有污迹的，涂糊的</td><td>smudged ink<br>糊掉的墨迹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The smudged address made the letter impossible to deliver.  糊掉的地址让这封信无法投递。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 soiled</td><td>/ˈsɔɪld/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>弄脏的，污损的</td><td>soiled linen<br>脏污的床单</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nurses collected the soiled sheets early every single morning.  护士每天清早收走脏污的床单。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stained</td><td>/ˈsteɪnd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有污渍的；着色的</td><td>a stained tablecloth<br>有污渍的桌布</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He hid the stained shirt at the back of the drawer.  他把有污渍的衬衫藏在抽屉最里面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sullied</td><td>/ˈsʌlid/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>被玷污的，受损的</td><td>a sullied reputation<br>被玷污的名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His sullied reputation never fully recovered after the scandal.  丑闻之后他被玷污的名声再也没有完全恢复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unclean</td><td>/ʌnˈkliːn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不洁的，脏的</td><td>unclean drinking water<br>不洁的饮用水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Many villagers still rely on unclean water from the river.  许多村民仍靠河里不洁的水生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 base</td><td>/ˈbeɪs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑劣的，低下的</td><td>base motives<br>卑劣的动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He acted from base motives of greed and envy.  他出于贪婪和嫉妒的卑劣动机行事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contemptible</td><td>/kənˈtemptəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可鄙的，卑劣的</td><td>contemptible behavior<br>卑劣的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cheating a blind beggar is a contemptible thing to do.  欺骗盲丐是可鄙的行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 despicable</td><td>/dɪˈspɪkəbl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人鄙视的</td><td>a despicable act<br>令人鄙视的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Stealing from a charity is a despicable way to make money.  从慈善机构偷钱是一种令人鄙视的赚钱方式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 detestable</td><td>/dɪˈtɛstəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>可憎的，令人厌恶的</td><td>a detestable habit<br>令人厌恶的习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He found the general's casual cruelty absolutely detestable.  他觉得那位将军随意的残暴极其可憎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dishonorable</td><td>/dɪˈsɑːnərəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不光彩的，可耻的</td><td>a dishonorable discharge<br>不光彩的除役</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Betraying an old friend for money is plainly dishonorable.  为钱背叛老友显然可耻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 execrable</td><td>/ˈeksɪkrəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>糟糕的，恶劣的</td><td>execrable table manners<br>极糟糕的餐桌礼仪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hotel served execrable coffee at breakfast every morning.  酒店每天早餐提供的咖啡都糟糕透了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ignominious</td><td>/ˌɪɡnəˈmɪniəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可耻的，不光彩的</td><td>an ignominious defeat<br>可耻的失败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The season ended in ignominious defeat at home.  这个赛季以主场可耻的失利告终。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mean</td><td>/miːn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑贱的；吝啬的</td><td>a mean little room<br>简陋卑贱的小屋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The family lived in a mean little room above a noisy printing shop.  一家人住在嘈杂印刷店楼上一间简陋的小屋里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vile</td><td>/vaɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>丑陋的；（道德上）可鄙的，卑鄙的</td><td>a vile insult<br>卑劣的侮辱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He shouted a vile insult and was quickly escorted outside.  他喊出一句卑劣的侮辱，很快被带了出去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wretched</td><td>/ˈretʃɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极差的；沮丧的</td><td>wretched living conditions<br>恶劣的居住条件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Families lived in wretched conditions beside the flooded riverbank.  一些家庭在泛滥的河岸边过着极差的生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 clean</td><td>/ˈkliːn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>干净的；打扫，清洗</td><td>a clean white shirt<br>干净的白衬衫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Keep the wound clean and change the bandage every day.  保持伤口干净，每天更换绷带。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 immaculate</td><td>/ɪˈmækjələt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>洁净无瑕的；完美无缺的</td><td>an immaculate record<br>毫无瑕疵的记录</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After thirty years of service his record remained absolutely immaculate.  服务三十年后，他的记录依然毫无瑕疵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 spotless</td><td>/ˈspɑːtləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>一点污迹也没有的</td><td>a spotless kitchen<br>一尘不染的厨房</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her spotless kitchen shone under the bright evening light.  她一尘不染的厨房在明亮的夜灯下发亮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stainless</td><td>/ˈsteɪnləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无污渍的；不锈的</td><td>stainless steel pots<br>不锈钢锅具</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The stainless surface showed not one mark after cooking.  烹饪后这块不锈表面没有一点痕迹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unsoiled</del></td><td>/ˌʌnˈsɔɪld/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>未被弄脏的，洁净的</td><td>unsoiled bandages<br>洁净未污的绷带</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only one unsoiled napkin remained by the end of dinner.  晚餐结束时只剩一张未弄脏的餐巾。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unstained</td><td>/ʌnˈsteɪnd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无污渍的；清白的</td><td>an unstained record<br>毫无污点的记录</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He retired after forty unstained years of public service.  他在四十年毫无污点的公职之后退休。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unsullied</td><td>/ʌnˈsʌlid/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>未被玷污的，纯洁的</td><td>an unsullied reputation<br>未被玷污的名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her unsullied honesty made her the obvious choice for treasurer.  她未受玷污的诚实使她成为财务的当然人选。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 honorable</td><td>/ˈɑːnərəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>光荣的，可敬的</td><td>an honorable retreat<br>光荣的撤退</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She made the honorable choice and returned all the money.  她做出可敬的选择，把钱全退了回去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lofty</td><td>/ˈlɔːfti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>崇高的；自大的</td><td>a lofty tone<br>自大的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She dismissed our idea with a lofty wave of her hand.  她自大地一挥手就否定了我们的想法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 noble</td><td>/ˈnoʊbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>高贵的，贵族的</td><td>a noble bearing<br>高贵的气派</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The horse moved with a noble grace across the field.  那匹马以高贵的优雅姿态穿过田野。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 straight</td><td>/ˈstreɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>直的，笔直的</td><td>a straight road<br>笔直的道路</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Drive straight ahead until you reach the second traffic light.  一直往前开，直到第二个红绿灯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 upright</td><td>/ˈʌpraɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>正直的，诚实的；竖直的</td><td>an upright citizen<br>一位正直的公民</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors described him as an upright and reliable man.  邻居们说他是个正直可靠的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 venerable</td><td>/ˈvenərəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>古老而受尊敬的</td><td>a venerable institution<br>历史悠久的机构</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The venerable library has served scholars for over four centuries.  这座古老而受人尊敬的图书馆已为学者服务了四百多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 virtuous</td><td>/ˈvɜːrtʃuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>品德高尚的，正直的</td><td>a virtuous life<br>高尚的生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He led a quiet, virtuous life and helped his neighbors freely.  他过着平静高尚的生活，乐于帮助邻里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sound</b></td><td>/saʊnd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>牢固的；健康强壮的；（逻辑）严谨的</td><td>sound financial advice<br>稳妥的理财建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her lawyer gave sound financial advice that saved the family thousands.  她的律师给出稳妥的理财建议，为家里省下数千元。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bombproof</td><td>/ˈbɑːmpruːf/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无懈可击的；防轰炸的</td><td>a bombproof argument<br>无懈可击的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer built a bombproof case from the documents.  律师用这些文件构建了无懈可击的案情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
