<tr><td>↳近 fast</td><td>/fæst/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>迅速的，快的</td><td>a fast runner<br>跑得快的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is a fast worker who finishes every job ahead of schedule.  他做事很快，每项工作都提前完成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 firm</td><td>/ˈfɜːrm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>坚定的，坚固的</td><td>a firm decision<br>坚定的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She remained firm despite enormous pressure from the board.  尽管董事会施加巨大压力，她依然坚定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stalwart</td><td>/ˈstɔːlwərt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>坚定忠诚的；强壮的</td><td>a stalwart supporter<br>坚定的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She remained a stalwart supporter of the project through every setback.  历经挫折，她始终是这个项目的坚定支持者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 strong</td><td>/strɔːŋ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>强壮的，坚固的</td><td>strong arms<br>强壮的臂膀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bridge is strong enough to carry heavy trucks.  这座桥足够坚固，能承载重型卡车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sturdy</td><td>/ˈstɜːrdi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>强健的，结实的</td><td>a sturdy wooden table<br>结实的木桌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sturdy wooden table stood in the middle of the farmhouse kitchen.  农舍厨房中间摆着一张结实的木桌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bouncing</td><td>/ˈbaʊnsɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>活泼健康的，精力充沛的</td><td>a bouncing baby boy<br>健康活泼的男婴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave birth to a bouncing baby boy last spring.  去年春天她生下一个健康活泼的男婴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fit</td><td>/ˈfɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>相符，适合；合适的</td><td>fit the description<br>符合描述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The suspect they arrested did not fit the witness's description.  他们逮捕的嫌疑人并不符合证人的描述。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hale</td><td>/heɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>强壮的，健壮的</td><td>hale and hearty<br>身体强健，精神饱满</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At eighty he remains hale enough to walk five miles a day.  八十岁的他依然强健，每天能走五英里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 healthy</td><td>/ˈhelθi/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>健康的，有益健康的</td><td>a healthy diet<br>健康的饮食</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Walking daily is a healthy habit at any age.  每天散步在任何年龄都是健康习惯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hearty</td><td>/ˈhɑːrti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>健壮的；热情的</td><td>hale and hearty<br>健壮结实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At eighty he remains hale and hearty.  八十岁时他依然健壮结实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 robust</td><td>/roʊˈbʌst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精力充沛的；强壮的，健康的</td><td>robust good health<br>强健的好身体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At eighty he remains robust enough to work in the garden daily.  他八十岁了，身体依然强健，每天还能在园中劳作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 well</td><td>/ˈwel/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>副词、形容词</td><td>好地，顺利地；良好的</td><td>go well<br>进展顺利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Everything went well until the printer broke down at midnight.  一切都很顺利，直到午夜打印机坏了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 well-conditioned</td><td>/wel kənˈdɪʃnd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>身体强健的，状态良好的</td><td>a well-conditioned athlete<br>体能良好的运动员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only well-conditioned runners finished the race in that heat.  在那样的高温下只有体能良好的跑者跑完了比赛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wholesome</td><td>/ˈhoʊlsəm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有益身心健康的</td><td>wholesome home-cooked food<br>有益健康的家常饭菜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children grew strong on wholesome food and plenty of fresh air.  孩子们靠有益健康的食物和充足的新鲜空气长得很结实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 analytic</td><td>/ˌænəˈlɪtɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>善于分析的，缜密的</td><td>an analytic mind<br>善于分析的头脑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her analytic mind quickly exposed the flaw in the plan.  她善于分析的头脑很快找出了计划的漏洞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coherent</td><td>/koʊˈhɪrənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>连贯的，条理清晰的</td><td>a coherent argument<br>条理清晰的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was too tired to give a coherent account of the accident.  他太累了，无法条理清晰地讲述事故。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consequent</td><td>/ˈkɑːnsəkwənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>合乎逻辑的；随之发生的</td><td>consequent upon the decision<br>由该决定引起的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The delays were consequent upon a shortage of parts.  这些延误是零件短缺造成的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rational</td><td>/ˈræʃnəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>理性的，合理的</td><td>a rational decision<br>理性的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Try to make a rational decision instead of an emotional one.  试着做理性而非情绪化的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reasonable</td><td>/ˈriːzənəbəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>合理的，通情达理的</td><td>a reasonable request<br>合理的请求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That seems like a perfectly reasonable request.  那似乎是完全合理的要求。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sensible</td><td>/ˈsensəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明智的，合理的</td><td>a sensible choice<br>明智的选择</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wearing boots was a sensible choice in that mud.  在那种泥地里穿靴子是明智的选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 valid</td><td>/ˈvælɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有效的；有根据的</td><td>a valid objection<br>有根据的反对意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Your ticket is valid for travel on any weekday this month.  你的票在本月任何工作日出行均有效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 well-founded</td><td>/ˌwɛl ˈfaʊndɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有充分根据的</td><td>a well-founded fear<br>有充分根据的担忧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their fear of another flood proved to be well-founded.  他们对再次洪水的担忧被证明有充分根据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 well-grounded</td><td>/wel ˈɡraʊndɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有充分根据的；基础扎实的</td><td>a well-grounded fear<br>有充分根据的担忧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their suspicion about the contract proved well-grounded after all.  他们对合同的怀疑最终证明是有根据的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ramshackle</td><td>/ˈræmʃækl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>摇摇欲坠的</td><td>a ramshackle barn<br>摇摇欲坠的谷仓</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They stored hay in a ramshackle barn that leaned toward the road.  他们把干草存放在一座向马路倾斜的摇摇欲坠的谷仓里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ailing</td><td>/ˈeɪlɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>生病的，衰弱不振的</td><td>an ailing economy<br>不振的经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The government pledged to rescue the ailing steel industry.  政府承诺拯救不振的钢铁业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 diseased</td><td>/dɪˈziːzd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>患病的，有病的</td><td>a diseased liver<br>患病的肝脏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The surgeon removed the diseased tissue from the lung.  外科医生切除了肺部的病变组织。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ill</td><td>/ˈɪl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>祸害，弊病；生病的</td><td>social ills<br>社会弊病</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report lists the ills afflicting the health system.  报告列出了困扰医疗系统的种种弊病。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sick</td><td>/ˈsɪk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>生病的，不健康的</td><td>fall sick<br>生病</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Half the crew fell sick during the long voyage.  在漫长的航行中一半船员生病了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 illegitimate</td><td>/ˌɪlɪˈdʒɪtəmɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不合法的；不合理的</td><td>an illegitimate conclusion<br>不合理的结论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Drawing that conclusion from such data is illegitimate.  从这样的数据得出那个结论是不合理的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 illogical</td><td>/ˌɪˈlɑːdʒɪkəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不合逻辑的，不合理的</td><td>an illogical conclusion<br>不合逻辑的结论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It seems illogical to cut staff while demand keeps rising.  在需求持续上升时裁员似乎不合逻辑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incoherent</td><td>/ˌɪnkoʊˈhɪrənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>语无伦次的，不连贯的</td><td>an incoherent argument<br>前后不连贯的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was so upset that his explanation became incoherent.  他太难受了，解释变得语无伦次。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 inconsequent</del></td><td>/ɪnˈkɑːnsəkwent/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不合逻辑的，前后不连贯的</td><td>an inconsequent argument<br>不合逻辑的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His inconsequent reasoning failed to convince the committee.  他不合逻辑的推理未能说服委员会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 invalid</td><td>/ɪnˈvælɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无效的；站不住脚的</td><td>an invalid argument<br>站不住脚的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract is invalid because it was never signed.  这份合同无效，因为从未签署。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 irrational</td><td>/ˌɪˈræʃənəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不理性的，无理性的</td><td>an irrational fear<br>非理性的恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He has an irrational fear of elevators and always takes stairs.  他对电梯有非理性的恐惧，总是走楼梯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unreasonable</td><td>/ənˈriːznəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不合理的，过分的</td><td>unreasonable demands<br>不合理的要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Asking staff to work three weekends running is unreasonable.  要求员工连续三个周末加班是不合理的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unsound</td><td>/ənˈsaʊnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可靠的，有缺陷的</td><td>unsound reasoning<br>有缺陷的推理</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The engineer judged the old bridge structurally unsound.  工程师判定这座旧桥结构不牢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 weak</td><td>/ˈwiːk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>脆弱的，虚弱的</td><td>a weak structure<br>脆弱的结构</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bridge has a weak structure and cannot bear trucks.  这座桥结构脆弱，无法承载卡车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sparse</b></td><td>/spɑːrs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>稀疏的，稀少的</td><td>sparse vegetation<br>稀疏的植被</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Vegetation grows sparse as the trail climbs above the tree line.  小径爬上林线以上后，植被变得稀疏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 exiguous</del></td><td>/ɪɡˈzɪɡjuəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>极少的，微薄的</td><td>an exiguous budget<br>少得可怜的预算</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They survived a whole year on exiguous rations of rice.  他们靠少得可怜的米粮过了整整一年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 meager</td><td>/ˈmiːɡər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>贫乏的</td><td>a meager income<br>微薄的收入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They raised four children on a meager income from a small roadside shop.  他们靠路边小店微薄的收入养育了四个孩子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 niggardly</td><td>/ˈnɪɡərdli/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的，少得可怜的</td><td>a niggardly raise<br>少得可怜的加薪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offered a niggardly raise after record profits.  在创纪录盈利后公司给了少得可怜的加薪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 poor</td><td>/pʊr/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>贫瘠的，贫乏的；贫穷的</td><td>poor rocky soil<br>贫瘠的石质土壤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The harvest was poor after a whole summer without rain.  整个夏天没下雨，收成很差。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scant</td><td>/skænt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不足的，缺乏的</td><td>scant attention<br>很少的关注</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report paid scant attention to the concerns of local residents.  报告对当地居民的关切几乎没有给予关注。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scanty</td><td>/ˈskænti/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺乏的，稀少的</td><td>scanty evidence<br>证据不足</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Evidence for the theory remains scanty.  支持该理论的证据仍然很少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scarce</td><td>/ˈskers/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>稀缺的，不足的</td><td>in scarce supply<br>供应短缺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fresh water became scarce during the drought.  旱灾期间淡水变得稀缺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 skimpy</td><td>/ˈskɪmpi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不足的，少得可怜的</td><td>a skimpy portion of meat<br>少得可怜的一份肉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report gave only skimpy evidence for its bold claims.  报告只给出很少的证据来支持大胆的结论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slender</td><td>/ˈslendər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>苗条的，细长的</td><td>a slender waist<br>纤细的腰身</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She held the flower by its slender stem.  她捏着那朵花细长的茎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slim</td><td>/ˈslɪm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>微小的，微薄的；苗条的</td><td>a slim chance of success<br>极小的成功机会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We have only a slim chance of catching the last train.  我们赶上末班车的机会很小。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sparing</td><td>/ˈsperɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>节俭的，节约的</td><td>sparing use of salt<br>节省地用盐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was sparing with salt because of his doctor's strict orders.  遵医嘱，他用盐十分节省。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stingy</td><td>/ˈstɪndʒi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>小气的，吝啬的</td><td>stingy with money<br>在钱上很吝啬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is stingy with money but surprisingly generous with his time.  他在钱上吝啬，在时间上却出奇大方。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abundant</td><td>/əˈbʌndənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>大量的，丰富的</td><td>abundant evidence<br>大量的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Water was abundant after the spring rains.  春雨过后水源充足。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ample</td><td>/ˈæmpəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>充足的，富裕的</td><td>ample opportunity<br>充足的机会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is ample room for everyone in the van.  车里有足够的空间容纳所有人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bountiful</td><td>/ˈbaʊntɪfəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>丰富的，充裕的</td><td>a bountiful harvest<br>丰盛的收成</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The valley gave them a bountiful crop of apples.  这个山谷给了他们丰盛的苹果收成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 copious</td><td>/ˈkoʊpiəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>大量的，丰富的</td><td>take copious notes<br>做大量笔记</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She took copious notes during every lecture and reread them each night.  她每堂课都记大量笔记，并在每晚重读。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 generous</td><td>/ˈdʒenərəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>丰富的，大量的；慷慨的</td><td>a generous helping<br>满满的一份</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She poured a generous glass of wine for each guest.  她给每位客人倒了满满一杯酒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 liberal</td><td>/ˈlɪbərəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>思想前卫的；慷慨的，大方的</td><td>liberal social views<br>前卫的社会观念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His liberal social views often surprised the older members of the club.  他前卫的社会观念常常让俱乐部里年长的成员感到意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 plenteous</del></td><td>/ˈplɛntiəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>丰富的，充足的</td><td>a plenteous supply of water<br>充足的水源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That year brought plenteous rain and unusually heavy crops.  那年雨水充足，庄稼格外茂盛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 plentiful</td><td>/ˈplentəfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>丰富的，大量的</td><td>a plentiful harvest<br>丰收</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rain came early, so the harvest was unusually plentiful.  雨来得早，所以收成异常丰富。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 10

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>spartan</b></td><td>/ˈspɑːrtn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>简朴的，节约的</td><td>spartan living conditions<br>简朴的生活条件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monks lived in spartan rooms with only a bed and a lamp.  僧侣们住在只有一床一灯的简朴房间里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 austere</td><td>/ɔːˈstɪr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>朴素的，朴实无华的</td><td>an austere room<br>简朴的房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He slept in an austere room with a bed and one chair.  他睡在一间只有一张床和一把椅子的朴素房间里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plain</td><td>/ˈpleɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>完全的，十足的；朴素的，清楚的</td><td>plain stupid<br>简直愚蠢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ignoring the warning was just plain stupid.  忽视警告纯粹是愚蠢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 simple</td><td>/ˈsɪmpəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>简单的，容易的</td><td>a simple task<br>简单的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Follow these simple steps and the machine will restart.  按这些简单步骤操作，机器就会重启。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stark</td><td>/ˈstɑːrk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严酷的，荒凉的；鲜明的</td><td>a stark contrast<br>鲜明的对比</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The photograph shows the stark landscape of the desert.  这张照片展现了沙漠荒凉的景观。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deluxe</td><td>/dəˈlʌks/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>豪华的，高级的</td><td>a deluxe suite<br>豪华套房</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They booked a deluxe suite overlooking the harbor.  他们订了一间可俯瞰港口的豪华套房。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 luxurious</td><td>/lʌɡˈʒʊriəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>奢侈的</td><td>a luxurious hotel suite<br>豪华的酒店套房</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They spent their anniversary in a luxurious hotel suite overlooking the harbor.  他们在一间俯瞰港口的豪华酒店套房里度过了结婚纪念日。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 plush</td><td>/plʌʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>奢华的，豪华的</td><td>a plush hotel suite<br>豪华的酒店套房</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They spent their anniversary in a plush hotel suite overlooking the sea.  他们在一间俯瞰大海的豪华酒店套房里度过结婚纪念日。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>spat</b></td><td>/spæt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>（小的）争吵</td><td>a spat over money<br>为钱的小争吵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The couple had a brief spat over money and then apologized.  这对夫妻为钱小吵一场，随后互相道歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 altercation</td><td>/ˌɑːltərˈkeɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>激烈争吵，口角</td><td>a heated altercation<br>一场激烈的争吵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A brief altercation over parking escalated into a lawsuit.  一场关于停车的短暂争吵最后升级成了诉讼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 controversy</td><td>/ˈkɑːntrəˌvɜːrsi/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>争论，争议</td><td>spark a controversy<br>引发一场争议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The painting sparked a controversy that lasted for decades.  这幅画引发了持续数十年的争议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disagreement</td><td>/dɪsəˈɡriːmənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>分歧，不一致，争执</td><td>a sharp disagreement<br>激烈的分歧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A long disagreement over funding stalled the whole project.  关于资金的长期分歧使整个项目停滞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dispute</td><td>/dɪˈspjuːt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>争论，争端；对…提出质疑</td><td>settle a dispute<br>解决争端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The neighbors are still in dispute over the fence.  邻居们仍在为那道围栏争执。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imbroglio</td><td>/ɪmˈbroʊlioʊ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>困境，复杂的局面</td><td>a political imbroglio<br>政治上的乱局</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The minister resigned in the middle of a diplomatic imbroglio.  部长在一场外交乱局之中辞职了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quarrel</td><td>/ˈkwɔːrəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>争吵，口角</td><td>pick a quarrel<br>找人吵架</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The brothers had a bitter quarrel about their father's will.  兄弟俩为父亲的遗嘱激烈争吵了一场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 squabble</td><td>/ˈskwɑːbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>为琐事争吵；口角</td><td>squabble over money<br>为钱争吵不休</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children squabble endlessly about who sits by the window.  孩子们为谁坐窗边没完没了地吵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tiff</td><td>/tɪf/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>小争吵</td><td>have a tiff with him<br>和他闹小别扭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She had a silly tiff with him about the dinner plans.  她和他为晚餐安排闹了一场无谓的小别扭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wrangle</td><td>/ˈræŋɡl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>纷争，争端</td><td>a legal wrangle<br>一场法律纷争</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A long legal wrangle over the land delayed construction for years.  一场关于这块土地的漫长法律纷争使工程推迟数年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amity</td><td>/ˈæməti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>友好关系，亲善和睦</td><td>a treaty of amity<br>友好条约</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two nations signed a treaty of amity and commerce.  两国签署了友好通商条约。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 concord</td><td>/ˈkɑːŋkɔːrd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>一致，和睦</td><td>live in concord<br>和睦相处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty brought decades of concord between the two states.  该条约给两国带来数十年的和睦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 harmony</td><td>/ˈhɑːrməni/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>和谐，融洽</td><td>live in harmony with<br>与…和谐共处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two communities have lived in harmony for generations.  这两个社区世代和睦相处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rapport</td><td>/ræˈpɔːr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>和睦，友好</td><td>build a good rapport<br>建立良好关系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new teacher quickly built a good rapport with her difficult class.  这位新老师很快与她难带的班级建立了友好关系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rapprochement</td><td>/ˌræproʊʃˈmɑːn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>和解，恢复友好</td><td>a slow rapprochement<br>缓慢的和解</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Trade talks began a cautious rapprochement between the two capitals.  贸易谈判开启了两国首都间谨慎的和解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>spate</b></td><td>/speɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>大量</td><td>a spate of complaints<br>大量投诉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The store received a spate of complaints about the faulty heaters.  商店收到大量关于取暖器故障的投诉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abundance</td><td>/əˈbʌndəns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>丰富，大量</td><td>an abundance of fresh fruit<br>大量新鲜水果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The market offered an abundance of fresh fruit at low prices.  市场以低价供应大量新鲜水果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bundle</td><td>/ˈbʌndəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>匆忙送走，急送</td><td>bundle him into a car<br>把他匆匆塞进车</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guards bundled the prisoner into a waiting van.  卫兵把犯人匆匆塞进等候的货车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dozen</td><td>/ˈdʌzən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>许多，一打</td><td>dozens of complaints<br>数十起投诉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Dozens of complaints arrived within the first week.  第一周内就来了数十起投诉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 multiplicity</td><td>/ˌmʌltəˈplɪsəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>多样性，多重性</td><td>a multiplicity of causes<br>多重原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The failure resulted from a multiplicity of small errors.  这次失败源于多种小错误的叠加。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 myriad</td><td>/ˈmɪriəd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无限的，大量的</td><td>myriad possibilities<br>无数的可能</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new technology opens myriad possibilities for teaching students in remote villages.  这项新技术为教偏远村庄的学生打开了无数可能。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 plentitude</del></td><td>/ˈplɛnɪtud/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>充足，大量</td><td>a plentitude of talent<br>人才济济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The region enjoys a plentitude of water and fertile soil.  该地区拥有充足的水源和肥沃的土壤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 profusion</td><td>/prəˈfjuːʒn/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>丰富，大量</td><td>a profusion of flowers<br>繁花盛开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A profusion of wildflowers covered the hillside after the spring rains.  春雨过后，山坡上开满了大量野花。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scad</td><td>/ˈskæd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>大量，许多</td><td>scads of money<br>大量的钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He earned scads of money from that single invention.  他靠那一项发明赚了大量的钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shipload</td><td>/ˈʃɪˌploʊd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>一船的量；大量</td><td>a shipload of grain<br>一船谷物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A shipload of grain reached the harbor before dawn.  黎明前一船谷物运抵港口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slew</td><td>/sluː/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>大量，许多</td><td>a slew of problems<br>一大堆问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new policy generated a slew of complaints from local business owners.  新政策引来当地商户的一大堆抱怨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 volume</td><td>/ˈvɑːljum/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>大量；容量；卷</td><td>a great volume of traffic<br>巨大的车流量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bridge cannot handle this volume of traffic.  这座桥承受不了如此大的车流量。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wealth</td><td>/ˈwelθ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>大量，丰富；财富</td><td>a wealth of experience<br>丰富的经验</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old editor brought a wealth of experience to the team.  这位老编辑给团队带来了丰富的经验。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ace</td><td>/ˈeɪs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>高手，能人</td><td>an ace at chess<br>国际象棋高手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is an ace with numbers and finished the audit alone.  他是数字高手，独自完成了审计。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bit</td><td>/ˈbɪt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>一点，少量</td><td>a bit of luck<br>一点运气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Add just a bit of salt to the simmering sauce.  往煮着的酱汁里加一点点盐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 driblet</del></td><td>/ˈdrɪblət/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>少量，点滴</td><td>in driblets<br>一点一点地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 News of the accident arrived in driblets all evening.  事故的消息整晚一点一点地传来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 glimmer</td><td>/ˈɡlɪmər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>少量，一丝；微光</td><td>a glimmer of hope<br>一丝希望</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The letter offered a faint glimmer of hope.  那封信带来一丝微弱的希望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 handful</td><td>/ˈhændˌfʊl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>少数，一把</td><td>only a handful of guests<br>只有少数客人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a handful of guests stayed until the end.  只有少数客人留到最后。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hint</td><td>/ˈhɪnt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>暗示，提示</td><td>drop a hint<br>透露一点暗示</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave me a subtle hint about the surprise party.  她微妙地暗示了那个惊喜派对。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mite</td><td>/maɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>微小的东西，很少的钱</td><td>a mite of comfort<br>一点点安慰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His brief letter offered a mite of comfort during those difficult winter months.  他简短的信在那些艰难的冬月里带来了一点点安慰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mouthful</td><td>/ˈmaʊθˌfʊl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>一口；一大堆话</td><td>a mouthful of water<br>一口水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He swallowed a mouthful of cold water and kept running.  他喝了一口凉水，继续奔跑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 nip</td><td>/nɪp/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>小口吃喝</td><td>a nip of whisky<br>一小口威士忌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He poured himself a nip of whisky and sat down by the fire.  他给自己倒了一小口威士忌，坐到火边。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ounce</td><td>/ˈaʊns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>少量，一点；盎司</td><td>every ounce of strength<br>每一分力气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He used every ounce of strength to lift the beam.  他用尽每一分力气抬起那根梁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pittance</td><td>/ˈpɪtns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>少量津贴</td><td>work for a pittance<br>为微薄报酬工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The apprentices worked long hours for a pittance and no benefits.  学徒们长时间工作，只拿微薄津贴且没有福利。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sprinkle</td><td>/ˈsprɪŋkəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>少量，星星点点</td><td>a sprinkle of sugar<br>少量的糖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cook added only a sprinkle of sugar to the batter.  厨师只往面糊里加了少量的糖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 trace</td><td>/ˈtreɪs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>痕迹；微量</td><td>a trace of irony<br>一丝讽意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tea showed only a trace of bitterness.  这茶只有一丝苦味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>sleek</b></td><td>/sliːk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>时髦的，优美的</td><td>a sleek silver car<br>线条优美的银色轿车</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She drove a sleek silver car that turned heads on every street.  她开着一辆线条优美的银色轿车，一路引人注目。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chic</td><td>/ˈʃiːk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>时髦的，潮的</td><td>look effortlessly chic<br>显得自然时髦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She looked effortlessly chic in a plain black dress.  她穿着一件素黑连衣裙，显得自然时髦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fashionable</td><td>/ˈfæʃənəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>时髦的，流行的</td><td>a fashionable neighborhood<br>时尚的街区</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Long coats became fashionable again after that film came out.  那部电影上映后长外套又流行起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trendy</td><td>/ˈtrendi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>时髦的，赶潮流的</td><td>a trendy neighborhood<br>时髦的街区</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old docks have become a trendy neighborhood full of galleries.  旧码头已变成满是画廊的时髦街区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 voguish</del></td><td>/ˈvoʊɡɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>时髦的，流行一时的</td><td>voguish jargon<br>流行一时的行话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His essay is packed with voguish terms that mean very little.  他的文章塞满了意义不大的时髦术语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>specious</b></td><td>/ˈspiːʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>似是而非的，欺骗性的</td><td>a specious argument<br>似是而非的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His specious argument collapsed as soon as the data were examined.  一查数据，他似是而非的论证立刻站不住脚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 beguiling</td><td>/bɪˈɡaɪlɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迷人的；有欺骗性的</td><td>a beguiling but false promise<br>诱人却虚假的承诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His beguiling manner hid a thoroughly calculating and selfish nature.  他迷人的举止掩盖了极其精于算计的自私本性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deceitful</td><td>/dəˈsiːtfəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>欺诈的，骗人的</td><td>deceitful practices<br>欺诈的做法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The firm was fined for deceitful advertising practices.  该公司因欺诈性广告做法被罚款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deceiving</td><td>/dɪˈsiːvɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>欺骗的，骗人的</td><td>a deceiving appearance<br>骗人的外表</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The calm surface was deceiving, for the current ran fast.  平静的水面骗人，水流其实很急。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deluding</td><td>/dɪˈluːdɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>欺骗性的，使人误信的</td><td>a deluding promise<br>骗人的承诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Those deluding advertisements cost investors millions.  那些骗人的广告让投资者损失数百万。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 delusive</del></td><td>/dɪˈluːsɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>欺骗性的，虚妄的</td><td>a delusive hope<br>虚妄的希望</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He clung to the delusive hope of a sudden rescue.  他抱着突然获救的虚妄希望不放。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 delusory</del></td><td>/dɪˈluːsəri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚妄的，具欺骗性的</td><td>a delusory sense of safety<br>虚妄的安全感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty produced a delusory sense of lasting peace.  这项条约带来了持久和平的虚妄感觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fallacious</td><td>/fəˈleɪʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>谬误的，不合逻辑的</td><td>a fallacious argument<br>谬误的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer exposed the fallacious reasoning behind the entire claim.  律师揭穿了整个主张背后的谬误推理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 false</td><td>/ˈfɔːls/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚假的，不真诚的</td><td>a false smile<br>虚假的笑容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His false praise fooled nobody at the small dinner table.  他虚假的赞美在那张小餐桌上骗不了任何人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 misleading</td><td>/ˌmɪsˈliːdɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>误导的，使人误解的</td><td>a misleading headline<br>误导性的标题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report was criticized for its misleading statistics.  这份报告因误导性的统计数字受到批评。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aboveboard</td><td>/əˈbʌvbɔːrd/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无欺诈的，光明正大的</td><td>open and aboveboard<br>公开而光明正大</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole deal was entirely open and aboveboard.  整笔交易完全是光明正大的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 forthright</td><td>/ˈfɔːrθraɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>直率的，说话率真的；直接的，不绕弯子的</td><td>a forthright answer<br>直率的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His forthright answer surprised the reporters who expected evasion.  他直率的回答让原本预期他闪避的记者很意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 straightforward</td><td>/ˌstreɪtˈfɔːrwərd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>简单明了的；坦率的</td><td>a straightforward procedure<br>简单直接的流程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The instructions are straightforward enough for a child to follow.  说明简单明了，连孩子都能照着做。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>speck</b></td><td>/spek/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>小点，少量</td><td>a speck of dust<br>一粒微小的灰尘</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single speck of dust on the lens ruined the photograph.  镜头上一粒灰尘毁了这张照片。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ace</td><td>/ˈeɪs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>高手，能人</td><td>an ace at chess<br>国际象棋高手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is an ace with numbers and finished the audit alone.  他是数字高手，独自完成了审计。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bit</td><td>/ˈbɪt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>一点，少量</td><td>a bit of luck<br>一点运气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Add just a bit of salt to the simmering sauce.  往煮着的酱汁里加一点点盐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 driblet</del></td><td>/ˈdrɪblət/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>少量，点滴</td><td>in driblets<br>一点一点地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 News of the accident arrived in driblets all evening.  事故的消息整晚一点一点地传来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 glimmer</td><td>/ˈɡlɪmər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>少量，一丝；微光</td><td>a glimmer of hope<br>一丝希望</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The letter offered a faint glimmer of hope.  那封信带来一丝微弱的希望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 handful</td><td>/ˈhændˌfʊl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>少数，一把</td><td>only a handful of guests<br>只有少数客人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a handful of guests stayed until the end.  只有少数客人留到最后。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hint</td><td>/ˈhɪnt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>暗示，提示</td><td>drop a hint<br>透露一点暗示</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave me a subtle hint about the surprise party.  她微妙地暗示了那个惊喜派对。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mite</td><td>/maɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>微小的东西，很少的钱</td><td>a mite of comfort<br>一点点安慰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His brief letter offered a mite of comfort during those difficult winter months.  他简短的信在那些艰难的冬月里带来了一点点安慰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mouthful</td><td>/ˈmaʊθˌfʊl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>一口；一大堆话</td><td>a mouthful of water<br>一口水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He swallowed a mouthful of cold water and kept running.  他喝了一口凉水，继续奔跑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nip</td><td>/nɪp/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>小口吃喝</td><td>a nip of whisky<br>一小口威士忌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He poured himself a nip of whisky and sat down by the fire.  他给自己倒了一小口威士忌，坐到火边。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ounce</td><td>/ˈaʊns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>少量，一点；盎司</td><td>every ounce of strength<br>每一分力气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He used every ounce of strength to lift the beam.  他用尽每一分力气抬起那根梁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pittance</td><td>/ˈpɪtns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>少量津贴</td><td>work for a pittance<br>为微薄报酬工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The apprentices worked long hours for a pittance and no benefits.  学徒们长时间工作，只拿微薄津贴且没有福利。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sprinkle</td><td>/ˈsprɪŋkəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>少量，星星点点</td><td>a sprinkle of sugar<br>少量的糖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cook added only a sprinkle of sugar to the batter.  厨师只往面糊里加了少量的糖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trace</td><td>/ˈtreɪs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>痕迹；微量</td><td>a trace of irony<br>一丝讽意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tea showed only a trace of bitterness.  这茶只有一丝苦味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abundance</td><td>/əˈbʌndəns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>丰富，大量</td><td>an abundance of fresh fruit<br>大量新鲜水果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The market offered an abundance of fresh fruit at low prices.  市场以低价供应大量新鲜水果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bundle</td><td>/ˈbʌndəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>匆忙送走，急送</td><td>bundle him into a car<br>把他匆匆塞进车</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guards bundled the prisoner into a waiting van.  卫兵把犯人匆匆塞进等候的货车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dozen</td><td>/ˈdʌzən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>许多，一打</td><td>dozens of complaints<br>数十起投诉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Dozens of complaints arrived within the first week.  第一周内就来了数十起投诉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 multiplicity</td><td>/ˌmʌltəˈplɪsəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>多样性，多重性</td><td>a multiplicity of causes<br>多重原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The failure resulted from a multiplicity of small errors.  这次失败源于多种小错误的叠加。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 myriad</td><td>/ˈmɪriəd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无限的，大量的</td><td>myriad possibilities<br>无数的可能</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new technology opens myriad possibilities for teaching students in remote villages.  这项新技术为教偏远村庄的学生打开了无数可能。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 plentitude</del></td><td>/ˈplɛnɪtud/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>充足，大量</td><td>a plentitude of talent<br>人才济济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The region enjoys a plentitude of water and fertile soil.  该地区拥有充足的水源和肥沃的土壤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 profusion</td><td>/prəˈfjuːʒn/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>丰富，大量</td><td>a profusion of flowers<br>繁花盛开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A profusion of wildflowers covered the hillside after the spring rains.  春雨过后，山坡上开满了大量野花。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 scad</td><td>/ˈskæd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>大量，许多</td><td>scads of money<br>大量的钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He earned scads of money from that single invention.  他靠那一项发明赚了大量的钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 shipload</td><td>/ˈʃɪˌploʊd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>一船的量；大量</td><td>a shipload of grain<br>一船谷物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A shipload of grain reached the harbor before dawn.  黎明前一船谷物运抵港口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 slew</td><td>/sluː/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>大量，许多</td><td>a slew of problems<br>一大堆问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new policy generated a slew of complaints from local business owners.  新政策引来当地商户的一大堆抱怨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 volume</td><td>/ˈvɑːljum/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>大量；容量；卷</td><td>a great volume of traffic<br>巨大的车流量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bridge cannot handle this volume of traffic.  这座桥承受不了如此大的车流量。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wealth</td><td>/ˈwelθ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>大量，丰富；财富</td><td>a wealth of experience<br>丰富的经验</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old editor brought a wealth of experience to the team.  这位老编辑给团队带来了丰富的经验。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>spectator</b></td><td>/ˈspekteɪtər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>观众；旁观者</td><td>a crowd of spectators<br>一群观众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A crowd of spectators cheered wildly as the runners entered the stadium.  跑者进入体育场时，一群观众疯狂欢呼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bystander</td><td>/ˈbaɪˌstændər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>旁观者，看客</td><td>an innocent bystander<br>无辜的旁观者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two innocent bystanders were injured in the crash.  两名无辜的旁观者在车祸中受伤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 observer</td><td>/əbˈzɜːrvər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>观察者，观察员</td><td>a keen observer<br>敏锐的观察者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 As a keen observer of politics, she predicted the result.  作为敏锐的政治观察者，她预测了结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 onlooker</td><td>/ˈɑːnˌlʊkər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>旁观者，观看者</td><td>a curious onlooker<br>好奇的旁观者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Curious onlookers gathered around the street performer.  好奇的旁观者围在街头表演者周围。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 viewer</td><td>/ˈvjuːər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>观看者，电视观众</td><td>millions of viewers<br>数百万观众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Millions of viewers watched the final episode live.  数百万观众直播观看了最后一集。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 watcher</td><td>/ˈwɑːtʃər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>观看者，监视者</td><td>a patient bird watcher<br>耐心的观鸟者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Bird watchers waited hours for the rare heron to appear.  观鸟者等了数小时才见到那只稀有的鹭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 actor</td><td>/ˈæktər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>演员；行动者</td><td>a talented young actor<br>有才华的年轻演员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young actor stole every scene he appeared in.  这位年轻演员在他出现的每一场戏中都抢尽风头。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 performer</td><td>/pərˈfɔːrmər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>表演者，演出者</td><td>a street performer<br>街头表演者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The performers bowed as the audience rose to applaud.  表演者鞠躬致意，观众起立鼓掌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 player</td><td>/ˈpleɪər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>演奏者，选手，演员</td><td>the leading player<br>主要演员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Each player rehearsed the scene until midnight.  每位演员都把这场戏排练到午夜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>spectrum</b></td><td>/ˈspektrəm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>光谱；范围，系列</td><td>a broad spectrum of views<br>广泛的各种观点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The survey captured a broad spectrum of views on the proposed reform.  这项调查收集了对改革提案的各种观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 diapason</td><td>/ˌdaɪəˈpeɪzən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>音域，全音域；洪亮乐声</td><td>the full diapason of sound<br>完整的音域</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The organ filled the church with its deep diapason.  管风琴以其深沉洪亮的乐声充满教堂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gamut</td><td>/ˈɡæmət/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>全范围，全域</td><td>run the whole gamut<br>涵盖全部范围</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her novels run the gamut from comedy to tragedy.  她的小说涵盖了从喜剧到悲剧的全部范围。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scale</td><td>/ˈskeɪl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>标度，等级；规模</td><td>on a large scale<br>大规模地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rate your pain on a scale from one to ten.  请在一到十的标度上给你的疼痛评分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spread</td><td>/spred/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>传播，散布；蔓延</td><td>spread quickly through town<br>迅速传遍全城</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rumors spread through the office faster than the memo did.  谣言在办公室传得比通知还快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stretch</td><td>/ˈstretʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>延长，拉长</td><td>stretch out the meeting<br>把会议拖长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They stretched the two-day trip into a leisurely full week.  他们把两天的行程拉长成悠闲的一整周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>speculate</b></td><td>/ˈspekjuleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>推测，揣测</td><td>speculate about the cause<br>推测原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Scientists can only speculate about the cause of the sudden extinction.  科学家只能推测这次突然灭绝的原因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assume</td><td>/əˈsuːm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>就任，承担（职权）；假定</td><td>assume office<br>就职上任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She will assume office as director next Monday.  她将于下周一就任主任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conjecture</td><td>/kənˈdʒektʃər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>推测，猜测；（没有依据地）认为</td><td>pure conjecture<br>纯属推测</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Without records, any date for the painting is pure conjecture.  没有记录，这幅画的年代纯属推测。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 daresay</td><td>/ˌderˈseɪ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>料想，敢说</td><td>I daresay he will come<br>我料想他会来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I daresay the results will surprise a few people.  我料想这些结果会让一些人吃惊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imagine</td><td>/ˌɪˈmædʒən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>想象；猜想，认为</td><td>hard to imagine<br>难以想象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I imagine the meeting will run past six o'clock.  我猜会议会开到六点以后。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 presume</td><td>/prɪˈzuːm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>推测，假定；擅自做</td><td>presume innocence<br>假定无罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We must presume the letter was lost in the post.  我们只能假定那封信在邮寄中丢失了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 suppose</td><td>/səˈpoʊz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>猜想，假定，认为</td><td>suppose for a moment<br>姑且假定一下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Suppose the train is late; how will you reach the airport?  假设火车晚点，你怎么去机场？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 surmise</td><td>/sərˈmaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（根据不足的）推测，揣测</td><td>a mere surmise<br>只是一种揣测</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Without documents, his account is a mere surmise about what happened.  没有文件，他的说法只是对当时情形的揣测。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 suspect</td><td>/səˈspekt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>推测，猜想；怀疑</td><td>suspect a hidden motive<br>猜想有隐藏动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I suspect the delay has nothing to do with weather.  我猜想这次延误与天气无关。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 prove</td><td>/ˈpruːv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>证明，证实</td><td>prove his innocence<br>证明他无罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New evidence finally proved his innocence after eleven long years.  新证据在漫长的十一年后终于证明他无罪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 substantiate</td><td>/səbˈstænʃieɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实</td><td>substantiate the claim<br>证实这一说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No evidence has emerged to substantiate his serious accusations.  没有证据能证实他严重的指控。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 validate</td><td>/ˈvælədeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实，确认；使有效</td><td>validate the results<br>验证这些结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Independent labs validated the results within three months.  独立实验室在三个月内验证了这些结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>spendthrift</b></td><td>/ˈspendθrɪft/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>挥霍者，败家子</td><td>a reckless spendthrift<br>肆意挥霍的败家子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A reckless spendthrift, he owed money to nearly every friend.  作为一个挥霍无度的人，他几乎欠了每个朋友的钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 fritterer</del></td><td>/ˈfrɪtərər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>浪费时间或金钱的人</td><td>a hopeless fritterer<br>无可救药的挥霍者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her brother is a fritterer who wastes both his savings and his talent.  她弟弟是个挥霍者，既浪费积蓄也浪费才华。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 profligate</td><td>/ˈprɑːflɪɡət/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>挥金如土的，挥霍的</td><td>profligate spending<br>挥霍无度的开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council's profligate spending on ceremonies angered local taxpayers.  议会在典礼上挥霍无度的开支激怒了当地纳税人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spender</td><td>/ˈspendər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>花钱的人；挥霍者</td><td>a big spender<br>花钱大手的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My uncle is a big spender who never checks his bank balance.  我叔叔花钱大手大脚，从不看银行余额。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 squanderer</del></td><td>/ˈskwɑndərər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>浪费者，挥霍者</td><td>a squanderer of talent<br>浪费才华的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 History remembers him as a squanderer of a vast family fortune.  历史把他记作败掉万贯家财的挥霍者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 waster</td><td>/ˈweɪstər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>挥霍者；无用之人</td><td>an idle waster<br>游手好闲的败家子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His father called him a waster who had thrown away every chance.  他父亲说他是个败家子，白白丢掉了每个机会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wastrel</td><td>/ˈweɪstrəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>肆意挥霍的人，败家子</td><td>a drunken wastrel<br>酗酒的败家子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The family fortune was spent by a wastrel who gambled nightly.  家族财产被一个夜夜赌博的败家子花光了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 extravagant</td><td>/ɪkˈstrævəɡənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>挥霍的；过度的，不必要的</td><td>extravagant spending<br>挥霍的开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their extravagant spending on parties left the charity nearly broke.  他们在宴会上的挥霍开支让这家慈善机构几乎破产。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 improvident</del></td><td>/ɪmˈprɑvɪdənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不节俭的；无远见的</td><td>improvident spending habits<br>不节俭的花钱习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of improvident borrowing left the family with nothing.  多年无节制的借贷让这家人一无所有。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 squandering</td><td>/ˈskwɑːndərɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>浪费的，挥霍的</td><td>squandering public funds<br>挥霍公款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Squandering money on gadgets left him broke by December.  在小玩意上挥霍钱财让他十二月就身无分文。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 thriftless</del></td><td>/ˈθrɪftləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不节俭的，浪费的</td><td>thriftless habits<br>浪费的习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His thriftless spending soon exhausted the family savings.  他不节俭的花销很快耗尽了家庭储蓄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unthrifty</del></td><td>/ʌnˈθrɪfti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不节俭的，挥霍的</td><td>an unthrifty heir<br>挥霍的继承人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The unthrifty young lord sold off the estate piece by piece.  这位挥霍的年轻贵族把地产一块块卖掉了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wasteful</td><td>/ˈweɪstfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>浪费的，挥霍的</td><td>wasteful of resources<br>浪费资源的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Printing every email is wasteful and completely unnecessary.  把每封邮件都打印出来既浪费又完全没必要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 economizer</del></td><td>/ɪˈkɑːnəmaɪzər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>节约者，节省用具</td><td>a careful economizer<br>精打细算的节约者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 As a lifelong economizer, she never wasted a coin.  作为终身节约的人，她从不浪费一分钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 penny-pincher</td><td>/ˈpɛni ˌpɪntʃər/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>吝啬鬼，一分钱都省的人</td><td>a notorious penny-pincher<br>出名的吝啬鬼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His uncle was a penny-pincher who reused envelopes.  他叔叔是个连信封都重复使用的吝啬鬼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 penny-pinching</td><td>/ˈpɛni ˌpɪntʃɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的，抠门的</td><td>penny-pinching habits<br>抠门的习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His penny-pinching ways annoyed everyone at the restaurant.  他抠门的做法让餐馆里所有人都不快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 provident</td><td>/ˈprɑːvɪdənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>节俭的；有远见的</td><td>a provident household<br>节俭有远见的家庭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A provident household sets money aside long before hard times arrive.  节俭有远见的家庭远在困难到来之前就存下钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 scrimping</td><td>/ˈskrɪmpɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>省吃俭用的，节省的</td><td>scrimping on food<br>在吃上省钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After years of scrimping, they finally bought a small house.  省吃俭用多年后，他们终于买了一栋小房子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 skimping</td><td>/ˈskɪmpɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>克扣的，偷工减料的</td><td>skimping on materials<br>在材料上偷工减料</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Skimping on safety checks eventually caused the accident.  在安全检查上偷工减料最终导致了事故。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 thrifty</td><td>/ˈθrɪfti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>节俭的</td><td>a thrifty shopper<br>节俭的购物者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thrifty habits helped her pay off the loan early.  节俭的习惯帮她提前还清了贷款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>
